KYNTIEN AI KHUBLEI
Nga dap da ka jingsngewnguh ia kine kisamla, u Banteilang
Singh Rumnong, M.Sc. (Agri) ba trei mynta kum u Lecturer ha
ka State Institute of Rural Development (SIRD), Meghzdaya bad

u Nathaniel J. D. Shadap, B. Sc. (Agri) ba trei mynta kum u
Farm Manager, Potato Experimental Station, Upper Shillong,
kiba ia pyllait por bad leh shitom ban khmih, ban pynthymmai,
ban pynbeit bad pynbit pynbiang ia kane ka kot rep kat kum
ka jingdawa ha ka jingkylla ka por.
Ha ka snem 1982-83 la don ki ba pynsngew ha i Rev. B. M.
Pugh (Bam kwai ha ling U Blei) ban shon biang ia kane ka kot
(namar khlem da shon shuh) ba ka long kaba myntoi ia ki
nongrep. Namar ba i la dkhat lade bad shongthait la bun snem
na ka kam hikai ha ka Kolej rep, shuwa ban shon biang ia
kane ka kot, i la pan jingiarap na kito ki rangbah kiba don ha
ka Agriculture Department jong ka Sorkar Meghalaya ha ka
ban pynthymmai bad pynbeit pynbiang kat kum ka jingdonkam
ka por. Hynrei namar ka jingteh ka kam ki khlem ioh por ban
leh kumta. Kumta i khlem lah ban pynmih ia kane ka kot ha ka
por ba i dang im wat la i la thoh bad pynbiang ia ka 'Tien
Lamphrang.
Nga kyrmen ba kane ka kot, KA JINGREP HA
MEGHALAYA, kaba la pynthymmai kan long ka jingiarap
bad jingmyntoi ia ki nongrep shi tyllup ka ri Khasi-Jaintia bad
ia ki nonghikai ha ki skul rep bad ha ki training centre.
Nga ai khublei bad nga pynpaw biang ia ka jingsngewnguh
ia kitei ki samla baroh arngut. To U Blei Trai Kynrad ruh Un
nang kyrkhu kyrdoh ba kin nang iaid shaphrang, bad ba kin
nang iai noh ia ki symboh jingtip jingshemphang ban nangroi
nangsan ka rep ka riang ban dap ban khiup, ban san ban kiew
ka khih ka kamai shityllup. Nga sngewdei ban ai khublei ruh ia
u Maqbool Lyngdoh, Senior Scientific Assistant, Tea
Experimental Station, Umsning, ia ka jingiarap jong u ha ka
lynnong kaba iadei bad u Slasha bad u Navin Bahl, u ba don
i
mynta ha Coffee Board, Shillong, u ba la ai ka jingiarap jong u

ha ka lynnong kaba iadei bad u Kopht. Nga ai khublei ruh ia i
Kong B. L. Mawlong i ba la iarap ha ka ban type ia kane ka kot
bad ruh ia baroh kito kiba la iarap kat ba lah ei la i bor.
Khublei
Sitimon Sawian
(Mrs. B. M. Pugh)
KA TIEN LAMPHRANG
Nga la thoh ia kawei ka kot ha ka ktien
Kheisi ha ka snem 1948 kaba nga la khot
KA KOT RER Kito kiba la pyndonkeum ia
ka ki la kyntu ia nga ba ngan pyntnih biang
ia ka neunar ba kam shym don shuh ha ki
dukan die-kot ia bun snem. Namar ba nga
la long u nonghikai lajan baroh shi lynter
ka jingim jong nga, ngam sngew lah ban
kiar na kane ka kam wat ia nga tip ba ka
long kaba khia, kham tarn ha kine ki snem
(1982-83) haba nga ia dap 85 snem ka rta
Khnang ban pynbiang ia katei ka jingkwah, la sngew
donkam ban thoh thymmai da kane ka kot kaba nga khot KA
JINGREP HA MEGHALAYA, kaba pyni ym tang ia ka jingkylla
jong ki por, hynrei kham tarn ia ka jingroi jong ka jingstetd ha
ka rep hi. Da kaba ngeit ba kane ka kot kan iarap kham tarn ia
kiba rep ha ri Khasi Jaintia, nga la thoh ia ka ha ka ktien Khasi,
ym ha ka English kaba long mynta ka Official State Language
jong ka Meghalaya. La kumta ruh nga kyrmen ba yn lah ban
pynkylla ia ka sha ka ktien Garo bad lehse sha kiwei pat ki
. ktien ’riewlum jong kito kiba shong ha kine ki thain jong ka ri
India.
Ka jingrep ka long ka kam kaba bun ki ju pyrkhat ba ka
long kaba ngi iatip baroh; hynrei ngi la tip mynta ba ka jingrep
ruh ka donkam ka jingnang-jingtip kaba bun syrtap ba yn ym
lah satia ban thoh lut ia ka ha kane ka kitab kaba rit. Ngan
pyrshang hangne tang ban iathuh ia kiei-kiei kiba nga ngeit

kin iarap ia u nongrep ha ri Khasi-Jaintia bad lehse ia kito kiba
ha kine ki ri lum jong ka North-East India lada yn lah ban
pynkylla ia ka sha ki ktien jong ki.
Uwei u briew uba stad, U Sir Joseph Hutchinson, uba la
long ha kawei ka por U Botanist bad Chief Geneticist jong ka
British Empire Cotton Growing Corporation, bad hadien pat u
Professor of Agriculture ha Cambridge University, England, u
iii
la ong, “Kam long kaba lah ia uwei u briew ban study ne wad
jingtip ka ban biang ia uno-uno uwei u jingthung wat lada uta
u briew u leh ia kata baroh shi rta ka jingim jong u” . U nongrep
uba $tad ruh un shem ba urn lah ban ong ba u long uba nang
batip hit shaphang ka rep. Nalor kata, ka jingstad shaphang ka
rep ruh ka nang roi bad, ngi tip, ba kane ka kot hadien katto-
katne snem kan ym long kaba bit ba biang ban pule ia ka. Ngi
kyrmen ba kane ka jingpyrshang ka jong ngi kan iarap ia kite
tuba rep ha kine ki por mynta.
Ha teba kut ngam lah khlem da pyni ia ka jingsngewnguh
eh jong nga ia kito kiba la shimti ban thoh ia kito ki lynnong,
kumba la pyni ha khlieh jong kawei-pa-kawei ka lynnong.
Shillong, 1983
Brandon Marpna Pugh.
Iv
LYNNONG 1
KA JINGDONKAM lA KA REP
Balei ngi wad jingstad ia ka ban rep
Khnang ba u briew un im suk im sain ha kane ka pyrthei, u
donkam la kumno-kumno ia ka bam kaba biang, ka phong ka
kup kaba biang bad ka iing ka sem ruh kaba biang. Na kine lai
kiba u briew u donkam eh ha ka jingim jong u, kitei ar tylli,
kaba nyngkong bad kaba ar, ki long kibit ngi ioh na ka rep. Ka
jingrep namar kata ka long kawei kaba u briew u dei ban leh;
bad ha kane ka pyrthei mynta ha kaba ki nongshong-shong ki
nang roi stet katne-katne, ka jingrep ka long shisha ka jingtrei

kaba u briew u dei ban trei.
Hynrei la shem ba ki ri kiba la iaid shaphrang ki don kham
khyndiat ki nongrep ban ia kito ki ri kiba sah dien. Ha ka pyrthei
kaba la roi bad la stad don bun Jait ki rukom jingtrei kiba pynmih
ia kiba bun ki jingdonkam jong u briew. Kumta ki nongrep ha
kito ki ri ki dei ban kham duna; bad khnang ban pyndap bam
ia kiwei pat ki nongtrei ha kano kano ka ri kaba la roi bad iaid
shaphrang ki nongrep ki ia dei ban long kiba nang bha ia ka
rep. Ha ri America (U.S.A.) uwei u nongrep u lah ban pynmih
ka bam ia ka ban bsa haduh kumba 29 ngut ki briew. Kumta
imat ka biang ban don tang saw ngut ki nongrep ha man la ka
shispah ban bsa ia ki para nong-America. Hynrei namar ba ka
ri America ka nang bad ka stad bha ha ka rukom rep, ka la lah
ban phah u kew, na uba ngi ioh u ata bad u moida, sha kylleng
ka pyrthei. Kumta ngi sngewthuh ba don kumba hynriew ngut
ki nongrep ha man la ka shispah u nong-America. Hynrei ha
kane ka ri India ka jong ngi, ki nongrep ki don haduh kumba
70 ne 80 na ka 100 ki nongshongshnong. Bad namar ba ka ri
jong ngi ruh ka la nangiaid shaphrang ha kiba bun ki kam ki
jam, ki nongrep kin sa nang kham duna bad ka jingnang jingtip
kumno ban rep ruh ka la dei ban nang roi. Dang khyndiat
1
snem mynta ba ka ri jong ngi da ka jingwad ia ki jingstad kumno
ban rep ka la lah ban pynmih ka bam kaba biang ia la u para
nong-India baroh. Ha ki por kiba mynshuwa ngi kum ka ri hi
baroh kawei ngi la leit wad ban pyndap jingbam na kiwei pat
ki ri.
Ka histori jong ka rep ha ka pyrthei
Ka jingtip ia ka rep, la iathuh ia ngi, ka sdang ynda haba u

briew, kumba 8000 snem mynshuwa, u iohi ba ki symbai jong
ki hiut ba u bam bad wan rah sha ka jaka shong ka jong u, ki
mih kum u jingthung uba thymmai haba la bret ia ki ha ka
khyndew ha ki aiom bad samoi kaba biang. Kine ki symboh
symbai, ym tang ba ki pynmih ki jingthung kiba thymmai, hynrei
ki pynmih ruh bun shah ki symbai kiba ki lah ban bam ynda u
jingthung u ia iaw bha.
Kane ka jingshem jong kito ki briew, ha ka jinglong
riewkhlaw jong ki, ka la long da shisha kaei kaei kaba la pynkylla
khluk ia ka jingim u briew ha kane ka pyrthei. Na kaba u la ju
im da kaba leit wad ia ki jingbam na ki madan phlang lane da
ki soh na khlaw lane da ka ba leit beh mrad ka la pynlong ia u
ban nang ban thung ki jingthung hajan ka jaka shong jong u
hi: ka la pynlah ia u ban pyntnih hi ha shen ia la ka bam.
Na kine ki phlang la mih hadien habud ki symboh-jingbam
(food-grains) kum u kew, u barii, u kba bad bun kiwei-kiwei.
Kito ki riew-hyndai ki la thung ruh ia kiwei-kiwei pat ki jingthung
na kiba la wan mih ki bun jait ki jhur bad ki soh ki pai jong ki
juk mynta.
Hooid, don haduh mynta ki jait-bynriew kiba im hi kum ha
ki por hyndai : kiba im da ki jingbam khlaw bad da kaba beh
mrad, hynrei kiwei pat te ki la nang iaid shaphrang.
Ki thain ha kiba la don ka jingrep kaba thikna ha ki por ba
dang lung ka pyrthei ki la long, katba ngi tip, ha Egypt, harud
jong ka wah Nile, bad ha Mesopotamia (Iraq), harud jong ki
wah Euphrates bad Tigris. Lajan ha kijuh ki por ruh la sdang
ia ka rukom rep kaba khambha ha China harud jong ka wah
Hwangho (Yellow River) bad ka wah Yangtzekiang, bad ha ri
2
India mh harud jong ka wah Indus bad ka wah Ganges, bad

lehse ha Southeast Asia (kata ha Bangladesh, kaba ju long
mynshuwa shibynta na ka ri India, ka Burma, Thailand bad
kiwei-kiwei ki ri).
Ka rukom rep ha ki por hyndai, kata shuwa ia ka 2(XX)-
1000 B.C. ruh ka la sdang ha ka rukom rep kaba iasyriem ia
ka kaba ngi dang leh haduh mynta ha ki katto-katne ki ri lum
jong kane ka ri. Kata, ki shu buh ne thep ia ki symbai ha ki
thliew kiba rit ba la shu tih da u dieng ne u siej uba nep. Ka
jingpyndonkam da ki tiar ba la thaw na ki kynja nar (metals)
lane ka nar (iron) hi ka wan suki-suki, katba u briew u nang
kham stad. Ha kito ki por ia ka kam rep hi la ju iehnoh ha ki
kynthei, katba ki shynrang ki leit ap ia ka shnong ka thaw ban
iada ia ka na ki nongshun.
Hadien-hadien, kum ha Egypt bad Mesopotamia ki briew
ki la nang kumno ban pyndonkam ia ka sboh; ban shna ki nur-
um ba ka um kan tuid sha ki lyngkha; ban ri ki jingri ki ban
ring ia ka lyngkor, kum ka ut, ka kada, ki kulai, ki masi bad
kiwei-kiwei ki jingri. Ki nong China bad ki nong India ruh ki la
ia tip ia kitei ki rukom rep ha kito hi ki hajar snem. Haba ia
nujor bad ki ri Mihngi, ha kito ki por ka ri Phareng (Europe) ka
dang sah shadien. Hynrei katba ki ri Sepngi ki nang iaid
shaphrang ha ka jingnang bad jingstad ban trei ha ki bun rukom
naduh ka spah snem kaba khadhynriew (A.D.) ki ri Mihngi pat
ki la shu sah hajuh. Ngi lah ban ong ba ka jingrep ha ka ri
India ruh ka la shu neh hajuh wat haduh ki sngi bad ki snem
kiba khadduh jong ka jingsynshar ka ri Bilat ia kane ka ri. Ki
phareng (British) ha ka por jong ka jingsynshar jong ki ia kane
ka ri ki la wad jingnang-jingtip tang shaphang kito ki jingthung

kiba ka donkam ka ri Bilat, kum u kynphad uba ki donkam na
ka bynta ki karkhana thain jain jong ki, lane u jingthung slasha
na ka bynta ki kper sla-sha jong ki nong Bilat kiba ki phah pat
ia u slasha sha ka ri jong ki bad sha kiwei-kiwei pat ki ri jong
ka pyrthei. Ym shym don ka jingpyrshang kaba thikha ban
wad kumno ban pynroi ia ka rep kba ne ka rep kew kiba long
ki jingban kiba kongsam jong kane ka ri. Ka jingsdang ban
3
pynroi ia ka jingstad ha ka rep ka la long naduh ba ka ri ka la
ioh ia ka jinglaitluid ha ka snem 1947.
Ka jingpyrshang tyngeh pat ban pynroi ia ka rep ka riang
ha kine ki thain ri lum jong ngi kiba shaphang mihngi ka ri
India ka la sdang ha ka snem 1975 ynda ka Indian Council of
Agricultural Research (ICAR) ka la pynieng ia ka Regional
Complex jong ka ICAR ha kine ki thain hi, bad ka la buh ia ka
headquarters jong ka ha Shillong. Mynta kane ka ophis ka don
ha Khwan. Naduh kata ka por la don ka jingtip kaba kham
thikna shaphang ka rukom rep ia ki jingthung kiba bun jait ha
kine ki ri lum. Ngi khmih lynti ba kane ka jingleh ka Sorkar
India kan iarap ia ki nongrep jong ngi ha Meghalaya ruh ba
kin nang iaid shaphrang.
4
LYNNONG 2
KI BYNTA U JINGTHUNG
Khnang ba ngin sngewthuh shaphang ka rep, ka donkam
ia ngi ban tip bad ban sngewthuh katto-katne ia ki bynta jong
u jingthung bad ia ka jingtrei kam jong ki. U jingthung, ngi dei
ban kynmaw, u long u jingthaw uba im; bad ka jingtrei jong u
ruh ka long kaba phylla katta-katta. Ha kane ka lynnong, ngan
kdew ia ki bynta kiba kham kongsan jong u jingthung bad batai
lyngkot ia ka jingtrei bad jingdonkam jong ki.
Haba shu kren lyngkot, u jingthung u long tang ma u uba
lah ban shna jingbam dalade ialade. U briew lane u mrad, ki

sim ki khniang bad kiwei-kiwei kim lah ban leh ia kata. U
briew u ioh ia ka jingbam ka jong u da kaba bam ia ki mrad, ki
sim, ki khniang, lane ia ki jingthung. Ki mrad, ki sim, ki khniang
ruh baroh ki leh kumjuh. Ka jingbam jong ki ka long da ki para
mrad lane da kiwei kiwei baroh kiba don jingim, bad da ki
jingthung, kum ki hiut ki hier, ki sla, ki soh, ki pai bad kiwei
kiwei . Hynrei u jingthung u shna hi ia la ka bam na ka Iyer
bad na ki katto-katne ki met tynrai (elements) kiba ki ioh na
ka khyndew, bad na ka um kaba ki ring na ka khyndew. Hooid,
don katto-katne ki jingthung kiba im da ka jingbam kaba ki ioh
na kiwei pat ki jingthung. la kiba kum kita ki jingthung la khot
ki mangkariang (parasite).
U jingthung u don hynniew tylli ki bynta kiba kongsan. Kita
ki long kine : (1) ki thied, (2) ka met bah, (3) ki tnat, (4) ki sla,
(5) ki syntiew, (6) ki soh, bad (7) ki symbai
Ki Thied
Don bun rukom ki thied jong ki jingthung. Don kito kiba
ka kam jong ki ka long ban pynskhem i?i u jingthung, khamtam
ia u jingthung ne u dieng uba kham met bah. la une u jait
thied ki khot u tap root. Une u ngam beit shapoh khyndew
bad bunsien u jrong lajan kat ka jingjrong jong ka met bah
5
jong u jingthung haior khyndew. Lymda u thied u jrong kumta,
kata u jingthung ianc u dieng ha khlaw ruh u lah ban kyllon ha
ka Jingbeh ka Iyer. Une u tap root u don ruh ki tnat syriem ia
ki tnat u met bah jong ki diengbah.
Don kiwei pat ki jait thied kiba ngi ju shem ha ki kynja
phlang, kum ha u kba, u kew, u riewhadcm, u pai bad kiwei
kiwei ki jingthung kiba long na ka jaid (family) jong ki phlang
(gramineae). la kinc ki jait thied ki khot ki fibrous root. Teng-

teng ki jingthung kiba na ka jait ki phlang ki pynmih ki thied
kiba kyrshan ia ki. la kinc phi lah ban iohi ha u riew-hadem. la
kinc ki jait thied ki khot ki brace nc prop root (kata, ki thied
kiba ai jingkyrshan ia u jingthung). Kinc ki thied ki mih na ki
mat jong u jingthung bad ki pynkynduh ialade ha ka khyndcw.
Ki don sa kiwei pat ki jait thied kiba shu mih bad ym da poi
haduh khyndcw, ki shu sah ha Iyer. Ia kinc ki khot ki aerial
root. Hynrei kine ruh ha ki katto-katne ki jait dieng ki poi haduh
khyndcw bad ki sa long kum u dieng met hi. Kum kinc ki jait
thied ngi ju iohi ha ka jri nc ka dieng banyan. Kito kiba la poi
ha ka Botanical Garden ha Calcutta ki la iohi ia unc u diengbah
uba ki thied jong u ki kyrshan ia ki tnat jong u.
Ka kam kaba kongsan jong ki thied ka long ban ring um na
ka khyndcw. Kane ka um ka tuid lyngba ka met-bah jong u
jingthung lane u dieng bad ka mih pat na ki sla. Kane ka um
ka long ruh kaba don bun jait ki jingbam kiba u jingthung u
donkam; kumta kane ka bam ruh ka poi sha ki bynta baroh
jong u jingthung. Kane ka jingbam kaba u jingthung u ioh na
ka khyndcw ka long kaba tang khyndiat eh. Kaba bun ka
jingbam jong U jingthung ka long kaba u ioh na ka Iyer kaba
wan lyngba ki sla. Kane ka jingbam na ka Iyer ka iasoh pat bad
ka um kaba wan kiew na ka khyndcw bad ka sa pynlong ia ka
bynta kaba kham bun jong uno-uno u jingthung. Ki thied kiba
ring ia kane ka um na ka bynta u jingthung ka wan pat lyngba
ki thied kiba rit kiba la khot ki root hairs nc ki theid shniuh.
Kine ki don tang ha kaba kut eh jong kito kiwei pat ki theid

kiba kham hch ia ki. la kine ki theid shniuh lah ban pyni
(demonstrate) da kaba buh katto-katne ki symbai riewhadem
6
ha ka kot shonsia kaba sngem bad sa tap ruh ia ki da ka kot
shonsia kaba sngem. Kine ki thied shniuh kin sa mih hadien
katto-katne sngi. Dei kine ki thied shniuh kiba ring ia ka um na
ka khyndew kaba sngem, bad ym dei ki thied bah ne thied
heh. Ka long kaba bha ban kynmaw ia kane namar haba ai
um ia ki jhur kum ia u sohsaw lane ia ki dieng soh ruh, kam
long kaba bha ban ai um ha ka trai dieng ne ha trai jong u
sohsaw . Kane ka um ka lah ban kit ki khniang jingpang bad
kan wallam jingpatig sha uta u jingthung ne u diengsoh. Dei
ban ai um ha ka jaka (zone) ha kaba don kine ki thied shniuh,
kata ha tduh eh jong ki thied kiba kham heh ban ia ki. Haba
phut ia uno-uno u jingthung khnang ban leit thung ia u shawei
pat, ka donkam ban kynmaw bha ba ym dei ban pynsiew ia
kine ki thied shniuh, namar lada ym don maki. u jingthung un
ym lah ban ring um na ka khyndew, bad kumta un iap stai.
Ka Met-bah
Ki met-bah (trunk) jong ki jingthung ki iapher shibun ha ki
bun rukoin. Ka met bah jong ki diengsoh kum u sohphan bad
u sohpieng ki long da shisha ka met-bah kaba da kat ka met-
bah. Namar ba kawei na ki kam jong ka met-bah ka long ban
kyrshan ia ki tnat bad ki sla kiba don ha uno-uno u jingthung.
Kumta ka met-bah ka dei ban long kaba khlain. Ngi iohi bun
lait ki met bah jong ki dieng, kum ki diengbah ha khlaw, ki
dieng bniap bad ki phlang. Hynrei wat ki phlang ruh ki long la
ka rukom-rukom. Don ki jait-phlang kum u siej kiba heh bha
oad ka met bah ruh ka heh bad khlain. U pai ruh u long u jait-

phlang uba khlain bad heh bun shah ia ki para-jait phlang.
rlynrei kiba bun na ki jingthung kiba ngi rep ha ki lyngkha ki
don ki met bah (stems) ki bym da khlain eh, kum u kba, u
<ew bad kiwei-kiwei. Kine ynda beh ka Iyer ruh bunsien ki
<yllon noh. Ki jait-dieng bah ki don ruh ka snieh-dieng (bark),
<aba iada ia ka met-bah. Ngi lah ban ithuh ia ka jait-dieng
3unsien tang na ka snieh-dieng ne snep jong ka met-bah.
Hynrei ka kam kaba khraw eh jong ka met-bah (trunk lane
item) ka long ban kyrshan ia ki kynja thied (veins) kiba don
ia ka met-bah kiba kyntiew ia ka um bad ki jingbam jong u

jingthung na ka khyndew sha ka met-bah bad sha ki tnat bad
ki sla jong u jingthung. Ka jingbarn kaba u jingthung u shna ha
ki sla pat ruh ka iaid arsut sha ka mct-bah bad shaduh ki thied.
Kine ki tyndong (conducting tissues) kiba ieh ia kane ka kain
ki long kiba rit bha kum ki shhiuh.
Ki Tnat
II kynja jingthung baroh uba jyrngam ki sla, la kumno
kumno, u don pat ki tnat kiba mih na ka met-bah. Ka kam
kaba kongsan eh jong ki tnat ka long ban kyrshan ia ki sla ha
kata ka rukom ba kin ioh sngi.
Ki Sla
Kaba bun eh ka jingbarn ka ba don ha uno uno u jingthung.
kham tarn jong kito kiba jyrngam ki sla, la shna ha ki sla. Kato
kaba jyrngam kaba don ha ki sla kaba ki khot ka klorophil
(chlorophyll) ka shim ia ka bor na ka sngi bad ka pyniasoh ia
ka kynja Iyer (gas) kaba ki khot ka karbon dai-oksaid (carbon
dioxide) bad ka pynkylla ia ka da ka jingkhleh bad ka um sha
kiba bun ki jait jingbarn kum u starch, ka shini bad kiwei-kiwei.
Sa kawei pat ka kam jong ki sla ka long ban pynmih noh shabar
ia ka um kaba u jingthung u ring da ki thied na ka khyndew.

Uwei pa uwei u jingshung ne u dieng u don la ki rukom
rukom sla ki ba ia pher ha ka dur. Bun sien ngi lah ban ithuh ia
u jingthung ne u dieng na ka dur jong ki sla ha u. Kumba ngi
la ong haneng, ki sla bunsien ki don ka rong kaba jyrngam,
hynrei teng teng ki don ruh kiwei pat ki rong kum kaba saw ba
stem bad kiwei kiwei. Don bun ki dieng kiba duh noh ki sla ha
ka por tlang. Ki dieng ne ki jingthung kiba ki sla jong ki ki sah
baroh shi snem, la khot ki evergreen, hynrei kito kiba hap ki
sla ha ka por tlang la khot decidious. Kine ki dieng ki sa mih
sla thymmai ynda la syiaid ha ka por pyrem.
Nga ngeit ki nongpule ki kynmaw ba ha ki sla ki don ki
thied kiba syriem ia ka snur thapbawa. Don ki jait sla ha kaba
kine ki thied i kumba ki iathain (netted lane dicot) bad don
ruh ki jait sla ha kaba kine ki thied ki long lynter-lynter (parallel
lane monocot). Ki sla tympew, ki sla sohpyriam, sla sohphoh
8
bad ki sla jong kiba bun ki jingthung ki don ki thied ki ba long
kuin ka snur thapbawa, ka jar ne iarong, hynrei ki kynja phlang
baroh ki don ki thied ki ba long lynter-lynter. Ha kaba kut kine
ki thied. bunsien don i kynja khyrdop iba ngi lah ban iohi tang
da kaba pundonkam da ka iit-tyndong ne microscope. la kine
ki khyrdop ki khot ha ka kticn phareng ki stomata. Dei kine
ki stomata ki ba pynmih noh ia ka um ka bym shim la
pyndonkam da ki sla jong u jingthung ban shna jingbam.
Ki Syntiew
la kiba bun ki briew ki syntiew ki long kiei-kiei kiba itynnad
bad pynitynnad ia ka mariang; hynrei ki don ka kam kaba khraw
ha kane ka pyrthei. Khlem ki syntiew ngin ym lah ban ioh ki
symbai na kiba ngi lah pat ban nang pynroi shuh-shuh ia uta u

jingthung. bad ngin ym ioh soh ruh. Hynrei ki dei ki symbai
kiba ngi ioh khamtam na ki jait phlang kum u kba, u kew, u
barli bad kiwei-kiwei ki jingthung, kiba long ki jingbam kiba
kongsan eh ha ka pyrthei.
Don la kumno-kurnno san tylli ki bynta kiba donkam eh ha
u syntiew. la ki bynta kiba habar eh jong u syntiew ki khot ki
sepal. Kine ki long ki sla kiba rit. kiba ker ne iada ia u syntiew
haba u dang lung. Kito ki bynta kiba pynitynnad ia u syntiew
la khot ki petal. Ki rong kiba bun jait jong ki ki khring ia ki
khniang, kum u ngap, ban leit sha ki. Katto-katne ki syntiew ki
don ruh ka jingiwbih kaba pynlong ia ki khniang ban leit sha
ki. Nangta pat don ki bynta ha u syntiew uba ki khot ki stemen
(stamen). Kine ki don u pui-pui (pollen) uba stem uba lada
un hap halor uwei pat u bynta jong u syntiew uba ki khot u
pistil, un iapoikha, u symbai bad u soh te kin sa nang-heh
nang-heh ha u bynta jong u syntiew uba ki khot pat u carpel.
Don pat ki jingthung kiba don ar jait ki syntiew ha uwei u
jingthung. Ngi pyniapher ia kine ki syntiew da kaba ong ba uta
uba don uto u pui-pui (pollen) u dei u, syntiew shynrang, bad
ia uto pat uba don u pistil bad u carpel la khot u syntiew
kynthei. Ki nongrep ki ithuh bha ia kine ki ar jait ki syntiew,
kum kito kiba don ha u pathaw, lane u sohkhia lane u peskot.

bad uto uba don kito ki tmain (kito ki dei ki pistil) bad ki symbai
kiba dang lung, u dei u syntiew kynthei. Ha u sohkynphor ruh
ki don ar jait ki syntiew kiba ngi lah ban pyniapher da kaba
jem.
Ha kane ka jingiapoikha ha ki jitigthung kaba ki khot

Ki Soh bad Ki Symbai
Ynda uto u pui-pui (pollen) u la hap, kumba ngi la kdew
haneng. halor u pistil, u carpel u la sdang ban nang heh haduh
ba un da long soh, bad hapoh uto u soh ki don kito ki symbai.
Don hateng hateng ki diengsoh ha kiba u soh u lah ban shu
heh hi khlem da don kane ka jingiapoikha (pollination). Ka
kam jong u soh ka long ban sumar ia u ne ki symbai kiba don
hapoh jong u haduh ba kin da long kiba bit ban thung pat
khnang ba kata ka jait jong uta u jingthung kan yrn duh noh
na kane ka pyrthei. Ki rong stem rong saw bad kiwei-kiwei ki
rong ha ki soh ka iarap ruh ia kane ka jingpynphriang ia ki
symbai jong kito ki soh, namar kine ki rong ki iarap ia ki sim ki
mrad bad ia u briew ruh ban leit kheit ia ki bad ban leit rah
kylleng-kylleng ia ki.
Ki symbai dieng ruh bad jong ki hiut-ki-nier ki iapher ha la
ki dur-ki-dur. Kito kiba donkam ia ka jingiarap ka Iyer ban kit
ia ki sha jngai ki lah ban don kum ki thapniang kiba kit ia uta u
symbai kham sha jngai, kum ki symbai kseh. Ki don ki hiut
kiba ki soh bad ki symbai ki snoh ha ki jain u briew lane ha ki
shniuh, ha ki snieh ki mrad, kum ki sohbyrthit, bad kiwei-kiwei.
Ki don pat kiba ki soh ryngkat bad u symbai, kum ha u jalynhiar
ne dandelion kiba don kum ka parachute khnang ba ka Iyer ka
lah ban kit ia ki kham jngai.

LYNNONG 3
Kl JAIT JINGTHUNG KIBA KHAMBHA
Ki jingthung kiba mynta, ki la mih na ki jingthung kiba mih
ha khlaw ne ha ki lyngkha phlang. Ki nongrep ha kylleng ka
pyrthei ki la jied pat na ki jingthung kiba mih ha kper ne ha
lyngkha jong ki ia ki jait jingthung kiba kham bha. Kumta ha ki
hajar bad ki spah snem kiba la leit, u nongrep u la lah -ban
pynmih (evolve) ym tang ki jingthung kiba pher ba pher na
ka bynta ban pynmih ki jaid jingbam kiba pher-ba-pher, hynrei
hadien haiden ki la lah ruh ban pynmih ki jait jong uta u
jingthung hi kiba nang khambha-khambha (improved
varieties). Kumta u nongrep ha kaba nyngkong u la lah ban
pynmih u kba na uba u lah ban ioh ia u khaw uba bit ban shet
bad ban bam, hynrei hadien hadien u la nanglah ban pynmih
bun jaid ki kba kiba khambha na ka bynta ka jaka jong u lane
uba kham bang ia u ban bam. Ha kane ka rukom la mih bun
jait ki jaid kba ha ka pyrthei. Don lehse mynta ym duna ia ka
10000 ki jait kba (rice varieties) ha ka pyrthei. Ha kajuh ka
rukom don ruh bun ki jait phan ha ka pyrthei baroh kawei.
Bad ka long kumta ia kiwei-kiwei ki jingthung ruh. Ki riewstad
ki la nang ialeh ban pynmih ki jait (variety) kiba nang kham
bha-khambha ha man la ki jaid jingthung kiba ngi don ha la
ka ri ka ri.
Kawei ka rukom kaba ki nongrep ki ju leh ka long ban jied

ia u jingthung uba bha tarn katba ki lah ban bishar ha lyngkha
bad ban buh symbai da uta u jait na ka bynta ban thung ha ka
snem kaba bud. la kane ka rukom ban pynroi ia u jait jingthung
uba kham bha ki khot ka pedigree method. Lada shu jied
pat na kiba bun ki jingthung tang ia kito kiba kham bha bad
buh symbai na ki, ia kata ka rukom jingjied ki khot maee
selection. Kane ka rukom ban buh symbai kab? ar ka long
lehse kaba kham jem bad kaba u nongrep u lah ban leh bad ju
12
leh ruh. Katei kaba nyngkong ka shim por bad lehse ka bha ba
ia kane yn leh da ki saiantis (scientist) ka Sorkar.
Kawei pat ka rukom kaba ki riewstad ki ju leh ban ioh da ki
jait jingthung kiba kham bha ka long da kaba wad jingtip na
kano ka ri ne ka thain jong ka pyrthei uta u jingthung u wan.
Ki leh ia kane namar ba ki tip ba ha ka jaka ha kaba u mih
nyngkong eh uno-uno u jingthung hangta ki don bun jait ki
jaid jong kito ki jingthung na kiba ki lah ban jied. Ki la leh
kumta na ka bynta u phan. Namar ba ki tip ba u phan u la mih
nyngkong eh ha ki lum Andes ha South America, kumta ki la
phah ia ki scientist jong ka rep ban leit wad ia ki jait phan kiba
iapher uwei na uwei. Ha ri India ia kiba kum kine ki phan la
leit thung ha ki lum Simla bad na kita ki la ialeh ban pynmih ki
jaid phan kiba khambha bad kiba lait na kiba bun ki jingpang
jong ki phan.
Nalor kitei ki rukom kiba ia ju pyndonkam ban pynmjh da
ki jait jingthung kiba kham bha, ki riewstad mynta ki la
pyndonkam ruh ia ka jingpyniapoikha ia uwei u jingthung uba
bha ha kano-kano ka Hang bad uwei pat uba kham bha ha

kawei pat. Kum ka nuksa, uwei u jaid jingthung u lah ban long
uba sohbha bad uwei pat u lah ban long uba lait na kano-
kano ka jingpang kaba ju pynjulor ia kita ki jingthung jong
kata ka jaid. Ha kane ka ri ki la leh kumta ia u pai (sugar cane),
bad ki la pynmih kumta bun jait ki pai kiba iadei bad ki bynta
kiba iapher-iapher jong kane ka ri. Ka kane ka rukom ki khot
hybridisation. Ka Sorkar ka la jied ia ka shnong Coimbatore
kaba sha South India na ka bynta ban pynmih kumta iaki pai
kiba nang khambha-khambha. La jied ia ka Coimbatore namar
ka long ka jaka kaba syaid bad ki pai hangto baroh ki phuh
syntiew. Ka jingiapynpoikha ia ki jingthung ka long, kumba'ta
ong kham shakhmat da kaba wan rah da ki pollen na uwei u
syntiew bad buh pat ha u pistil jong uwei pat u pai. Teng-teng
ki la pyniapoikha hapdeng u pai bad u siej lane kiwei pat ki
jingthung kiba dei hi ki kynja phlang khnang ban pynkhlain ia
u pai.
Hangne ha Meghalaya ki nongrep ki la ju iohsngew ia uta
13
li hybrid maize (u riewhadem shiteng jait). la une u riewhadem
ruh ki riewstad ki pynmih da kaba pyniapoikha uwei u

bad bah khun shibun. Kane ka jingpyniapoikha ka shim por
shibun namar dei ban da jied bha ia ki kmie ki kpa (parents),

jingiakhleh bad kiwei pat. la kane ki khot da ka ktien phareng
isolation. Kane ka jingpynia jngai ne isolation ka pher na uwei
u jingthung sha u wei pat.
Ngi kyrmen ba ki Sorkar kin wad lad ban pynphriang ia ka
jingthung riewhadem da une u hybrid maize, kumba ki leh ha
America bad kiwei-kiwei pat ki ri ha kaba ka jingrep ka la
nangiaid sha phrang. la kane ka kam dei ban leh hi da ka
Sorkar, namar ba ym lah ban buh symbai da ki khun jong une
u hybrid maize.
Kitei ki rukom kiba ngi la kdew haneng ki long kiba ki ju
pyndonkam ban pynmih (evolve) ki jingthung kiba kham bha
na ka bynta ka rep lum bad rep pynthor. Hynrei ha kaba pynmih
ki jaid soh kiba kham bha pat la ju kham pyndonkam da kito
ki rukom kiba ki khot kaba bod (budding) ne kaba krap
(grafting), lane da ksba shu kolom (layering) la kine ki rukom
ban pynbha ia ki jaid diengsoh yn sa batai kham bha ha ki
lynnong kiba shadien.

LYNNONG 4
KA SUIN BNENG BAD KA REP
Ka jinglong jong ka suin bneng (climate) , ha ka Hang ka
jingshit, jingkhriat, u slap, ka jingjrong ne jinglyngkot ka sngi,
ka ktah shibun eh ia u jingthung bad kumta ruh ia ka rep ka
riang. Ka jingbha jong ka rep ka riang ka shong eh, nalor kiwei
kiwei, halor ka jinglong jong ka suin bneng. Ban pynkylla ia ka
suin bneng katkum ka jingkwah jong ngi ka long kaba shitom
shibun. Namar katta ngi dei ban pyniahap ia ka rukom rep
jong ngi bad ka jinglong ka suin bneng ha ia ki jaka jaka la
jong. Dei na kane ka daw ruh ba ngi hap ban da sngewthuh

shai ia ka jinglong ka suin bneng khnang ban lah ban pynbha
shuh shuh ia ka rep ka riang jong ngi.
Ka Jylla Meghalaya ka long kawei na ki jaka ha pyrthei
kaba jur bha u slap hynrei ka jingiapher halor ka jingshit
jingkhriat jong kawei ka bynta na kawei pat ka iar shibun. Ka
jylla hi ka don ka jingiapher ha ka jingshit (temperature range)
naduh 20*^0 haduh 36"C. Shaphang ki thain rilum Khasi ki ba
jrong kumba 600-2000 mitar na ka sla jong ka duriaw (mean
sea level) ka suin bneng ka long kaba khriat. Ki bynta ba
hapdeng ki ri thor bad ri lum pat ki long ki ba kham syaid bad
ki ri thor jong ka Meghalaya ki dei ki bynta ki ba shit. Dei na
kane ka jingiapher jong ka suin bneng na kawei ka bynta sha
kawei pat ba phin shem ruh ka jingiapher ha ki jait jingthung
(vegetation). Kumba shu ai nuksa phin shem ba ki dieng kseh
kiba iadei ha ki jaka ba kham khriat ki long bha ha rilum Khasi
bad ki dieng sal kiba lah ban kham shah ia ka jingshit ki mih
sha ri Bhoi.
U Slap bad ka rep ka riang.
U slap u long u ba donkam eh na ka bynta ka rep, bad ha
Meghalaya hi haduh mynta ngi dang shaniah beit tang ha'ka
um slap na ka bynta ka rep ka riang jong ngi. Ha kawei ka

Ka Jlngshit Jtngkhriat
Ka jingshit jingkhriat (temperature) ruh ka dei kawei na

Ka angi
Ngi dei ban sngewthuh ba lada slap eh ruh ym long bad
lada ikhiang eh ruh ym long. Ngi donkam shibun eh ia ka sngi

jingsngem jong ka Iyer bad ka jaka ruh ka jur bad kane ka

Ka Lyer
Ka lyer ruh ka long ka ba donkam bha ia ki jing thung

LYNNONG 5
K1 JAID KHYNDEW HA MEGHALAYA
Namar ba ka khyndew ka long kawei na kiei-kiei kiba ngi
pyndonkam eh ha ka rep, kumta ka donkam ban sngewthuh
katba lah ia ka khyndew bad ia ka jingiapher jong kawei na
kawei pat. Ha kaba nyngkong ka bha lehse ba ngin sngewthuh
ba ka khyndew kaba ngi rep mynta ha ki lyngkha jong ngi kam
shym long kajuh ha ki hajar snem ne wat ki spah snem kiba
mynshuwa. Ka la shim por bun-bun snem ba kan long kumba
ka long mynta.
Kawei na kine ki khyndew kiba ngi don mynta ha ki lum, ki
lyngkha bad ki pynthor jong ngi ki lah ban long kito kiba la
kylla khyndew na ki maw bad ki mawsiang kiba don hapoh
jong ki. la kine ki jaid khyndew ki ong ki residual soils. Ki
jingthung kiba mih nyngkong eh ha ki mawsiang ki long kito
kiba shu ai rong lieh, saw, ne jymgam ha uno-uno y mawsiang.
Kito ki long ki jingthung kiba rit kiba ha badei badei ym lah
ban iohi ia ki marwei-marwei da ki khmat, hynrei kiba ngi lah
ban iohi da ka iit tyndong kaba pynheh ia ki kaba ki khot ka
microscope. Kine ki dei ki bacteria bad ki fungi, kiba lah ban
im bad nangroi haba ki hap ha kano-kano ka jaka kaba ailad
ia ki ban roi. Kine ki lah ban pynkylla ia ki maw bad ki mawsiang
sha ka khyndew kaba bit ban thung jingthung ynda ki la trei
kumta da ki hajar bad ki phew hajar snem. Kum kine ki
khyndew ki long kiba ngi ju shem ha ki lum lane ha ki lum

madan (plateau) ha Meghalaya. Kine ki khyndew kim da long
kiba da sboh eh, namar ki jingbam kiba u jingthung u donkam
(plant nutrients) kiba ki ki don, ki long tang kito hi kiba la ju
don ha uta u maw ne u mawsiang, bad na ki hiut ki hier bad ki
jingthung kiba la ju long hedor jong ki ynda ki la pyut. Ka
jingsboh lane ka jingseisoh jong kine ki khyndew ka shong eh
shibun ha ka jingbun jong kane ka pyut ka syar na ki hiut ki
hier bad ki jingthung kiba la mih halor jong ki. Ynda haba
18
kane ka pyut ka la bun, ka khyndew bunsien ka la nang khsMnn
long kham long. Katba ka khyndew long ka nang rben katta
nih ka nang kham sboh bad kham long kaba seisoh.
Kawei pat ka jaid khyndew kaba ngi ju shem ha Meghalaya
hajan jong ki jaka riat ka long kaba ki khot ha ka ktien phareng
ka colluvial soil. Kane ka long ka khyndew kaba la shu hap
mian pa mian namar ka jingkhia jong ka hi. Ngi iohi ia kine ki
jaid khyndew ha ki thain Riwar. Kane ka khyndew ka iapher
na katei kaba haneng, ka residual soil, namar ba kam shym
sah hajuh hynrei la pynkynriah ia ka da ka jingkhia jong ka.
Kane ka khyndew ka kham long khyndew maw lane ka
khyndew kaba la iakhleh bad ki maw; kumta ka long ka
khyndew kaba ka um ka ngam bha ha ka (well>clrafned). Kane
ka khyndew ruh kam da long kaba seisoh bha, lymda ka long
ka khyndew ha kaba ki dieng ki siej ki la shu mih hangto da ki
bun bun snem khlem pynkhih ia ki (virgin soil). Ka jinglong
phrew-phrew jong kine ki jaid khyndew ka iarap ruh ia ka um
ban nym neh ha ka hynrei ban ngam sha kaba jylliew jong ka
khyndew. Bun eh na ki jaid jingthung ba ngi thung ha ki kper
bad ki lyngkha kim sngewbha ia ka khyndew ktieh, lane kano-

kano ka khyndew ha kaba ka um ka sah hajuh. Kum kine ki
khyndew ha Meghalaya ki long kiba bit ban thung diengsoh
lane kino kino ki dieng kiba heh kiba ym donkam ban da
pynkhih ia ka khyndew kaba don hapoh jong ki. Kim long
namarkata ban thung ia ki jingthung kum u phan, u riewhadem
bad kiwei-kiwei kiba donkam ban kynroi ia ka khyndew bunsien
ha ka shi aiom.
Ki don pat kiwei ki jaid khyndew kiba kham bun eh ha ri
Dkhar, hynrei hangne ha Meghalaya ki don tang ha ki madan
kiba harud jong ki wah bad ki wah bah. Kine ki long ki khyndew
ba la wan buh da ki wah bad ki wah bah. la kane ka jaid
khyndew ki khot ka alluvium lane ka alluvial soil. Kine
bunsien ki long ki khyndew kiba seisoh bha; namar ki long
bunsien ki khyndew kiba la kit da ka um slap na ki lum, ki
ranab lum lane na ki khlaw ki btap. Namar ba ka khyndew
kaba halor eh jong ka sla khyndew ka long kaba bha na ka
19
bynta ki jingthung, kumta kane ka khyndew ia kaba la kit da
ka um ka long kaba seisoh. Kane ka khyndew ka la iakhleh
ruh bad bun kiwei-kiwei pat ki symboh khyndew. Kine ki
khyndew ki long kiba rew (coarse) haba ki don kham shajan
jong ka tlong jong ka wah; hyntei katba ki nangjngai ki long
kiba kham ni. Hyntei bunsien ki lah ban long kham rew ha
kawei ka syrtap bad kham ni ha kawei pat, bad kumta ter-shi-
ter. Kane ka long kat kum ka jingkhlain jong ka um kaba wan
bret ia ka khyndew na kawei ka por sha kawei pat. Ka um
kaba kham bt-lat ka buh tang ia ki symboh khyndew kiba kham
rew, hyndrei ka um kaba iaid suki ka iehnoh ia ki symboh
khyndew kiba kham ni. Ha kine ki alluvial soil la ju rep da u
kba lane uno-uno u jingthung uba long bha ha ki pynthor. Ha

ki jaka kiba kham duna u slap pat kum ha Punjab, ha Uttar
Pradesh bad kiwei-kiwei ki jylla kiba shaphang shatei lam
sepngi, ki thung bun jait ki jingthung kum u kew, u barli, u
phan, bun jait ki jhur, ki dai, u pai bad kiwei-kiwei. Kine ki jaid
khyndew ki long kiba da bit bha ban thung bun jaid ki jingthung.

LYNNONG 6
KI JINGBAM JONG U JINGTHUNG
Ka jingbam kaba bun eh jong u jingthung ka wan na ka
Iyer bad ka um. Ha ka Iyer ka don ka kynja Iyer (gas) kaba ki
khot ka carbon dioxide kaba long ka bih ia u briew bad u mrad
ruh hynrei kaba u jingthung u pyndonkam ban tei ia la ka met.
Kane ka gas ha ka jingshisha ka long kato kaba u briew bad u
mrad hi ki pynmih na ka jynhaw; ka dei ruh kato kaba mih na
ki motor kiba bun jaid ha kane ka pyrthei. Kane ka la don lypa
ha ka Iyer, kumba don ka oxygen bad ka nitrogen bad kiwei-
kiwei ki gas. Ka um pat ka rung ha u jingthung na ki thied jong
u. Da ka jintrei baphylla jong u jingthung kane ka carbon
dioxide ka iakhleh bad ka Iyer kaba rung ha ki sla da kita ki
thliew kiba rit kiba ngim lah ban iohi da ki khmat, kiba ki khot
ki stomata. Kine ki stomata ki longsyriem ia ki shynturu briew
hi. Haba kine ar ki iakynduh ha ka sla kaba jyrngam, ki kjat
sngi da ka jingiarap jong kato kaba jyrngam ha ki sla, kaba ki
khot ka chlorophyll ki pyniasnoh ia kito ar (ka um bad ka
carbon dioxide) bad ka pynkylla ia ki sha u starch lane ka shini
bad kiwei-kiwei ki jait jingbam kiba u jingthung u donkam ban
tei ialade. la kane ka rukom shna jingbam u jingthung ki khot
ha ka ktien phareng ka photosynthesis. Kumta ka jingbam
kaba u jingthung u ioh na ka khyndew ki long tang khyndiat
eh. Hynrei kine ki jingbam kiba wan na ka khyndew ki long

kiba donkam katta-katta ba u jingthung um lah ban long khkm
ma ki.
Ki jingbam kiba wan na ka khyndew na ka bynta u jingthung
ki long ka nitrogen, ka phosphorus, ka potassium, ka
calcium, ka sulphur (kyndok), ka hon (nar) bad ka
magnesium. Kine ki hynniew ki met tynrai, ryngkat bad kiwei
pat (lai) kiba wan na ka um bad ka Iyer (ka oxygen, ka
hydrogen bad ka carbon), ki long kiba u Jingthung baroh u
donkam khnang ba un san bad un heh. Nalor kine ki 10 Id met
21
tynrai kiba u jingthung u donkam eh, don shuh-shuh sa kiwei
pat kiba u jingthung u donkam kham khyndiat. Kine ki met
tynrai ki long ka manganese, ka zinc, ka boron, ka copper
(tama), ka chlorine, ka cobalt bad ka molybdenum. la kitei
hynniew kiba donkam kham bun la khot ki macro-nutrients,
bad ia kito hynniew kiwei pat kiba donkam kham khyndiat,
bad kiba don ruh kham khyndiat ha ka khyndew la khot ki
micro-nutrients. La kumta ruh ka jingiapher hapdeng ka iron
(ka nar) bad ka manganese ka long lajan kumjuh hi. U kba
(rice) uba shu antad, kata u bym da rnih bha eh u lah ban
donkam kumba shi kilo ka nar bad shi kilo ka manganese na
man la shi hectare ka khyndew.
Nitrogen
Ki khyndew rep Jong ngi ha Meghalaya ki long kiba duna
shibun ka nitrogen. Kane ka long kaei-kaei kaba phylla haba
ka nitrogen ka long ka kynja Iyer (gas) kaba don baroh sawdong
ia ngi. Ka nitrogen bad ka oxygen ki long ki bynta kiba bun eh
ha ka Iyer; ka nitrogen ka don haduh 78 bynta na ka shispah
bad ka oxygen ka don 21 bynta. Kumta kine ar ki long kumba
99 bynta na ka Iyer. Kiwei pat kiba don ha ka Iyer ki long ka
carbon dioxide (tang 0.03 per cent), ka argon, ka hydrogen
bad kiwei-kiwei. Kumta ka nitrogen kum ka Iyer ka don hi ha
ka khyndew ruh ha ki thliew (pores) kiba don hapdeng ki
symboh (particles) Jong ka khyndew. Hynrei khnang ba kan

long kaba u jingthung u lah ban pyndonkam, ka dei ban da
iasnoh bad kiwei pat ki met tynrai kum haba ka iasoh bad ka
potassium bad ka (nitrogen) bad pynlong ka potassium nitrate,
ne ha kaba ka iasoh bad u sodium bad ka nitrogen ban pynlong
ka sodium nitrate. Ka nitrogen ka lah ban iasoh bad kiwei kiwei
pat ki met tynrai bad kiba lah ban long ki jingbam jong u
jingthung. Hynrei namar ba kine ruh kim da biang eh, la
donkam ban ai da ki sboh bad ki sboh dawai kiba don ka
nitrogen. Ngin kdew hangne harum ia ki katto-katne ia kito
kiba lah ban ai ha ka khyndew ban pynroi ia ka nitrogen.
Ka eit ki mrad baroh ka long kaba don bun ka nitrogen. Ka
eit masi, ka eit kulai, ka eit sniang, ka eit langbrot ne eit biang.
22
ka eit syiar, bad ka kai bri (night soil). Hynjnsi ka jingbha jong
kine ki sboh baroh ka shong shibun eh ha ka rukom ba ngi
buh bad sumar ia ki. Ka eit mzisi kaba b pynbng bad ka um
jung meisi ka long kaba kham bha shibun (kata, kaba kham
bun ka nitrogen) ban ia ka sboh masi ha kaba ym shym b
sumar ban pyndonkam bad ka um jung masi artad. Ka um
jung masi ka don kumba arshah ka nitrogen ban ia ka eit masi;
hynrei namar ba ka eit masi ka bun kumba arshah ia ka um na
ka masi kumta bda pyniakhleh bng ia ka sboh eit masi ka
kham bha haduh ar shah ban ia ka sboh masi ka bym shym
don kane ka um na ka masi.
Kawei na ki rukom kaba ki ju leh ban sumar ia ka eit masi
ka long da kaba ki shna ka compost.
Katba nang bid ka por bad ki snem, ka jing pyndon kam b
ka sboh ieit kaba ngi ioh na ki jing ri ka la kham kiew namar ka
rep ka riang ha ka Ri Khasi Jaintia jong ngi ka la nang kham

khraw. Ka dei ruh imat ba ma ngi kum ki nong rep ngi sngew
thuh ba ngin ym bh ban pynmih ia uno uno u mar rep u ban
pynioh jingmyntoi khlem da pyndonkam b katei ka shoh.
Haba peit kumba iaid ka iew mynta ha ki bnai tbng, shi
trok lane kumba shiphew ne shiphew ton shiteng ka sboh masi
ka shong dor haduh kumba bi hajar phra spah haduh saw
hajar tyngka - Bad bunsien ka daw kumba long ha ki ri jong
ngi ka dei namar ba ka jngai eh ka jaka ri masi na ki jaka rep.
Kane ka long ka jingkit ka ba khia bad ha ki khep ki khep ka ju
pyndiaw mynsiem b u nongrep, khamtam haba u thmu ban
pynsan ia la ka jingtrei.
Ban bit na kine ki jing eh, ki don ki lad ki rukom ban shna
lane pynkhreh ia ki sboh, da kaba pyndon kam da ki phbng ki
hiut hi kynbat bad ki jingthung ba la kyntbt, kaba ka khyndew
jong ngi ka don kam, ban heh ka rep ka riang, bad ban pynduna
ia ka lut ka sep haba shu thied ia ka sboh kaba mih na ki jing
ri. la kane ka rukom shna lane pynkhreh ia kane ka sboh ki
khot ka composting.
23
Ngi tharai ka aiom kaba bha eh ban pynkhreh ia kane ka
compost hangne ha Ri Khasi Jaintia ka long ha ki por shiteng
snem kata ha ki bnai June -July. Ka dei ka por ha kaba ki
phlang ki hiut bad kiwei kiwei ki jyntang jingthung ba la kyntait
ki pahuh bha bad dei ruh ka por ba kine ki nuit ki pyut khloi.
Ngi dei pat ban jied ia ka jaka kaba kham hajrong khnang
ba kan ym lang ka um slap. Kane ka jaka kam dei ban jngai
ruh na ka um (water source). Hangta tih ka thliew kaba jylliew
kumba 2 phut haduh 3 phut (60-90cm) ka pynkiang, ka dei
ban long la kumno kumo 7 phut {210cm) bad ka lynter Jong

ka kumba 10 phut. (»300 cm) Nangta sa sdang pyndap ia kane
ka thliew da ki hiut ki phlang ki jingtang jingthung ba la kyntait
bad kiwei kiwei . la u kymbat u ba kham jwat dei ban da shain
shuwa ban pyndap ha ki ’liew sboh. Phriang syrtap haduh ban
da dap kumba 6 inch (15 cm) bad nalor kane sa ber da ka
sboh eit kaba dang im jong kino kino ki jingri kumba shi inch
eiei lane lah ban ber ruh da ka urea. Ngi leh kumne khnang ba
kito ki jingthung kiba rit ki ba don ha ka sboh jingri ki pyn
pyutkloi ia kito ki niut. lai leh kumtei, shi systap ki hiut ki
phlang shi syrtap ka sboh jingri, haduh ba kan da dap ka ’liew
sboh.Pyntam ia kane ka jing syrtap haduh kumba shi phut shi
phut shi teng na ka sla khyndew bad nangta sa set sbak ia ka
da kaba lape najrong da ka sboh jingri kaba la khleh lang bad
u khyndew.
la kane ka sboh ba la shna kumne lah ban pyndon kam
ynda la dap 100 haduh 150 sngi. Sha ki jaka ba kham syiad ka
kham pyut kloi ban ha ki jaka kiba kham khriat. Ha ki jaka ki
ba kham khriat haba ka jingpyut jong kine ki nuit ka kham
suki, dei ban da ai um hateng hateng ha ki por bym ioh slap
shuh.
Kito ki nongrep ki ba rep kham bun, ki lah ban shna kham
bun tylli kum ki ne ki ’liew sboh (compost pit) bad ban
pynduna ka por ka bor haka kit ka bah, ki lah ban shna ia ki
sha jan ki lynkha ne pynthor jong ki.
Kawei pat ka rukom kaba ngi lah ban leh ban pynroi ia ka
nitrogen ha ka khyndew ka long da kaba thung ki jingthung
24
kiba lah ban pyndonkam ia ka nitrogen kaba don ha ka Iyer
tia ka bynta jong ki hi. Kiba bun eh na kito ki jaid jingthung

kiba lah ban leh kumta ki dei kito ba ki khot ha ka ktien phaieng
ki legumes. Kine ki dei ki kynja ri ne rymbai. Ki syntiew jong
ki bunsien ki long ha ka dur ka thapbalieh (papileonaceous)
bad ki soh jong ki ki long ha ka dur jong u ri. U rymbai ktung,
u rymbai ja, u motor bad bun kiwei-kiwei pat ki dei kine ki
legume. la kine ki jingthung kiba na kane ka juh ka iing-kur
(family) lada phin tih sumar bad peit ia ki thied jong ki, phin
shem ia ki kynja sohkhlot (nodules). Ha kine ki soh-khlotdon
da ki lak bad ki phew lak ki jingthung kiba rit (bacteria) kiba
lah ban shim ia ka nitrogen na ka Iyer; kumta kine ki sohkhlot
hi ki long kiba dap da ka nitrogen, hynrei uta u jingthung ha
kiba ki mih ruh u long uba bun ka nitrogen. Ka jingtrei kaba
phylla jong kine ki bacteria ka long ba ki ai ia kane ka nitrogen
ha u jingthung, bad ki pat ki shim ia ka bor (energy) na ka
jingbam kaba ki bacteria ki ioh na uta u jingthung, kum ia u
starch ne ka kynja carbohydrate, bad da kata ka bor hi ki
wallam ia ka nitrogen ha ki jingthung. Kumta kito ki sohkhlot
kiba kum ki thapbasim ki long ki karkhana ban wallam ia kane
ka fertilizer (jingbam) kaba ki jingthung bad ki mrad baroh ki
donkam. la ki jingthung kiba ki da thung khnang na ka bynta
kane ka nitrogen ki khot ki green manures. Ha ri dkhar kum ha
Uttar Pradesh, Bihar bad kiwei-kiwei ki jaka ki shait thung
khnang da u saihon (sunn-hemp) . Ha ki jaka kiba kham bun
u slap kum ha W.Bengal ki shait thung da u dhaincha
(Sesbania aculeata). Ki kynja dai, ne ri, ne rymbai baroh ki
long kiba bit ban pyndonkam ha kane ka rukom. Don ruh kito
ki bym dei ki legume kiba lah ban pyndonkam kum ki green
manure, kum u rai (kynja tyrso-Brossfeo Juncea) lane wat u

syntiew cosmos ruh lane kino-kino ki jingthung kiba mih kloi,
kiba jem (succulent). Ha ri Bhoi u jingthung uba kiAssarheSe
ki khot u bogamedoloa {Tephrosla Candida) u lah ban long
uba bit ban pyndonkam ha kane ka rukom. la une u
bogamedoloa ha ki kper sha, ki thung ryngkat bad u dieng sh^
bad hadien lai snem u sa duh noh lane ki pom noh ia u.
25
la kine ki jingthung kiba ngi mut ban pyniong sboh dei ban
thung ha ka por ba dang shu hap slap malu-mala, bad ynda
hadien kumba lai bnai, pyndem ia ki bad sa iur, da kaba tap
lane iakhleh ia ki bad ka khyndew.
Kaba ai da ka sboh dawai ka long kawei pat ka nikom ban
ai nitrogen ia ki jingthung. Ka sboh dawai kaba kham kynrei
eh hangne ha Meghalaya mynta imat ka dei ka urea, la kane
ka sboh dawai lah ban ai shisien ne arsien ha u jingthung.
Halor ka urea don kiwei pat ki sboh dawai kum ka ammonium
sulphate ka sodium nitrate ka potassium nitrate bad ka
diammonium phosphate kiba ki ju pyndonkam khnang ban ioh
ia ka nitrogen. Hynrei ka sodium nitrate ka wan na shabar
kane ka ri jong ngi bad ka potassium nitrate ki pyndonkam ruh
na ka bynta ban shna bakhor.
Kawei pat ka nikom kaba ki nongrep ki lah ban leh ban ai
nitrogen ia ki jingthung ka long da u shaba uba mih ynda la
tylliat ia ki shyeing symbai, ban shim ia ka umphniang kaba
mih na ki, Kum u shaba na u symbai soh-ryndia (castor), u
shaba na u symbai shana budam, u symbai kynphad, u symbai
tyrso, bad kiwei-kiwei ki jait symbai kiba pynmih umphniang.
Hynrei namar ba kum une u shaba ne u kholi u long uba bit
ruh ban ai ia ki masi bad ki sniang, kumta busien la ju
pyndonkam na ka bynta ban aisboh tang da kito kiba kim
pyndonkam na ka bynta ki jingri, kum u shaba na u symbai

soh-ryndia, uba ki m2isi kim lah ban bam namar u lah ban don
katto-katne ka umphniang julab.
Phosphorus bad Potassium
Nalor ka nitrogen, ki jingthung ki donkam ruh ban ioh ia
ka phosphorus bad ka potassium (potash). Ka sboh shyieng
(bone<4neal) ka long kawei na kito ki sboh dawai ba ngi lah
ban ai ia ki jingthung. la kane ngi ioh na kaba shu tylliat ia ka
shyieng ki kynja mrad baroh. Kane ka don shibun ka
phosphorus bad katto-katne ka nitrogen ruh. Kawei pat ka sboh
kaba don ka phosphorus ka long ka superphosphate. Nalor ka
phosphorus kane ka sboh dawai ka ai ruh ia ka czdcium kaba
26
long kawei na ki met tynrai kiba ki jingthung ki donkam. fa ka
calcium lah ruh ban ioh na ka shun. Hynrei ia ka shun la ju ai
ban kham pynbha ia ka khyndew kaba skhah eh kum ka dew-
ktieh. Kawei pat ka sboh dawai kaba lah ban ai na ka bynta
ban ioh phosphorus ka long ka di-ammonium phosphate. Kane
ym tang ba ka ai ia ka phosphorus hynrei ka ai ruh ia ka nitrogen
kaba don kum ka ammonia ha ka.
Ki sboh dawai na kaba ngi lah ban ioh ia ka potassium ki
long ka potassium sulphate, ka potassium chloride bad ka
Muriate of Potash. U dpei ruh u long uba don shibun ka potash
na kaba ngi ioh pat ia ka potassium. Namarkata u dpei jong ki
dieng bad jong ki hiut ruh u long uba bun bha ka potash.
Katba ka phosphorus ka pynkhlain ia ki jingthung, kumta
ba u kba lane u kew ne kino-kino ki jingthung kiba iasyriem ia
kine kin kham duna kaba pat (lodge) haba jur Iyer, ne bun eh
ka urn, ne bun eh ka nitrogen, ka iarap ruh ia ki jingthung ban
kham lait na ki jingpang bad teng-teng ka kham pynitynnad
ruh ia ki. Ka potassium pat ka iarap ia ka jingbam kaba la shna
ha u jingthung ban leit sha kiwei-kiwei pat ki bynta jong u

jongthung hi (kum ka nuksa, ka jingbam ba u phan u shna ha
ki sla, ka leit sha ki phan kiba hapoh khyndew, bad kumta ki
nang-heh nang-heh), ka potassium ka iarap ruh ban kham
pynmih kham bun ka chlorophyll, kata kaba pynjyrngam ia ki
sla ha u jingthung, ka potassium ka iarap ruh ia ki symboh-
jingbam (grain), bad nalor kine baroh, ka potassium ka iarap
ban pynbeit ia kano-kano ka jingbym iaidbeit ha u jingthung
lada bun eh ka nitrogen bad ka phosphorus. Ha kaba kut ngi
dei pat ban kynmaw ba la u dpei u don shibun ka potassium,
kaba thang ia ki niut ki nier lane ia ki dieng ruh ka thang du^
noh ruh ia ka nitrogen kaba don ha ki bad ia ka humus (kynnoh
hiwmos) kaba sah ha ka khyndew da ka jingpyut jong ki dieng
bad ki sla dieng lane wat da ka jingpyut jong ki met-iap jong ki
mrad. Kane ka humus ka long kaba pynbha ia ki khyndew rep
baroh bad kaba pyniong ruh ia ki, bad kumta ngi tip ba ka
khyndew kaba kham iong ka long bunsien kaba kham seisbh
ruh.

Ha kaba pynwai ia kane ka lynnong ngi sngew ba ka dei ia
ki nongrep ban tip ruh ba don ki met tynrai kiba ki jingthung ki
donkam (lane lah ban donkam) tang khyndiat eh bad kiba ym
da kynrei ruh ha kano-kano ka khyndew. la kine ki khot ki
trace ne rare elements (ki met tynrai kiba ym da don kam
shibun). Kine ki long ka manganese, ka boron, ka zinc, ka

copper (tama), ka chlorine, ka aluminium, ka silicon, ka
molybdenum, bad ka calcium. Lada kine ki duna eh, ki
jingthung ki lah ban ioh jingpang lane kim da san bha.

khyndew ban pynmih ia u jingthung u ba bha. Mynta ka soikar
ka la seng ia ka jaka test khyndew ha jan man la ki distrik.
Ynda la dep test ia ka khyndew ki sa bthah de katno bad kumno
ban ai ia ka sboh mrad lane sboh dawai ba kiwei kiwei ki sboh
na ka bynta u jingthung.

LYNNONG 7
K1 TIAR REP
Lehse kan ym long kaba da bakla ch lada ong ba lah ban
ithuh ia ka jingstad u nongrep da ki tiar kiba u pyndonkam ha
ka rep. Khlem da leit sha jngai eh sha ki thain jong ka pyrthei
ha kaba ka jingrep ka sdang, ngi lah ban ong ba ha kine ki ri
lum jong ngi luh lah ban iohi ia ka rukom rep kaba iasyriem eh
bad kato kaba ki ju leh ha ki por hyndai ba ki ri kiba pawkhmat
mynta ki la iehnoh da ki hajar snem. Kawei kaba ngi iohi ha
kine ki ri lum kaba iasyriem bad kito ki rukom kiba rim ha
kiwei pat ki bynta jong ka pyrthei ka long ka jingpyndonkam
da u dieng ban thaw thliew ha ka khyndew ha kaba yn sa tep
ia u symbai. Ha kine ki ri lum jong ngi ki ju pyndonkam kham
bunsien da u siej ha ka jaka u dieng. Kumjuh ruh ynda u
nongrep u la sdang ban pyndonkam da u kynja mohkhiew, ha
ki por ba ka jingrep ka dang sdang, ia u mohkhiew ruh ia shna

na u dieng, kum na u dieng sning uba long uwei na ki dieng
kiba kham eh bad kham kynrei.
Ha Meghalaya, ha bun ki jaka mynta ki tiar rep kiba ngi ju
iohi ki long u mohkhiew bad ka wait. Ngim lah ban ong ba ngi
la iehnoh ia ka juk jong kine ki tiar ha kane ka juk mynta. Ka
jingpyndonkam ia ka lyngkor ha ka jaka u mohkhiew ka la
sdang shisha ha bun ki jaka. Ngi kyrmen ba ka jingpyndonkam
ia ka lyngkor kan nang kham roi katba ki sngi bad ki snem ki

LYNNONG • 9
U PHAN
U phan (Solonunt tubersum) u long u jingthung uba kham
kongsan ch ha ri Khasi-Jaintia namar kata ngin sdang shuwa
da une u jingthung.
U phan, kumba nga la ong ha ki lynnong kiba mynshuwa,
u long u jingthung u ba mih nyngkong ha South America. Imat
une u jingthung u la mih nyngkong nyngshap eh ha ar tylli ki
jaka ha katei ka ri, kawei ka long ha ki jaka lum jong ka ri Peru
bad Bolivia bad kawei pat ka long ha rud duriaw jong ka ri
Chile. Nangta ki lawan sha ka ri Europe, shen hadien ba la
shem ia ka ri America da u Christopher Columbus. Nangta pat
u sa phriang kylleng ka pyrthei. Shen hadien ba la wallam ia u
phan sha India, u Warren Hastings, u Lat Rangbah (Viceroy)
jong ka ri India ha ka por shynshar phareng, u la ioh katto
katne u symbai phan bad u la phah thung ha Sohra. Kata ka
long la sien ka nyngkong eh ba la thung phan ha ri Khasi-
Jaintia. Mynta ka jingthung phan ha kane ka ri jongngi ka la
roi haduh katta katta ba ka long kaba eh ia u Khasi-Pnar ban
pyrkhat ba laispah snem mynshuwa um juh tip ia une u
jingthung. Ka jingroi jongu ha ka pyrthei ruh ka long kaba
phylla namar u long mynta uwei na ki lai tylli ki jaid jingbam
ba kongsan tarn, hynrei namar ba don kham bun ka um ha u
phan ban ia u kew lane u khaw, kumta u kew bad u khaw ki
long, ha ka jingshisha, ki ba kham donkam ban ia u phan. Ka

pyrthei baroh kawei ka pynmih kumba 270 millian ton u phan,
595.5 millian ton u kew bad 529 millian ton u khaw. Ki san
tylli ki ri ki ba pynmih shibun eh u phan ki long kine : 1. Russia,
2. China, 3. Poland, 4. India bad 5. U.S.A. Ka India ka wan ba
saw ha ka jingkhlain ban pynmih phan.
U phan u long u jingthung uba long khambha ha ki jaka
khriat. Dei na katta ka daw ba ka Russia kaba long ka ri kaba
khriat ka wan ba nyngkong ha ka jingkhlain ban pynmih ia u
38
phan ban ia ka India kaba long ka ri kaba shit. Dei nih na kane
ka daw ba ha ri India ki juh rep phan ha ka por Hang sha kl
thain ri thor bad ha ka por lyuir ha ki thain ri lum. Teuigba kl
jaka ba shit kum ka Gujarat ka pynmih 24.55 ton shi hektafy ka
West Bengal ka pynmih 21 .97 ton shi hektar, katba Meghalaya
pat kaba long ka jaka khriat ka pynmih tang kumba 8.53 ton
shi hektar. Hoiod don kiwei pat ki daw nalor ka jingkhriat kiba
pynlong ia u phan ba un met bha ha kawei ka jaka b 2 ul met
sniew ha kawei pat. Kane ka shong ha ka jingstad jong ki
nongrep, ka jinglong ka khyndew bad kiwei kiwei pat ki daw
kum u symbai bad ki buit rep ba kham bha.
Ki jaid khyndew kiba bha eh ban rep phan ki long kito kiba
nga la khot ha ki lynnong ba mynshuwa ki loam, kata ki
khyndew kiba shiteng shyiap bad shiteng dewbyrtha (clay).
Ha ki khyndew kiba tang ka dewbyrtha u phan un ym lah ban
kyrsiep, hynrei ha ki khyndew shyiap ruh um da long bha namar
kine ki jait khyndew ki duna eh ka jingbam ba donkam na ka
bynta ki jingthung baroh. Ha ka ri Khasi-Jaintia, ka ri kaba
bun bha u slap, ka khyndew ruh ka dci ban long ka ba ym bat

eh ia ka um namar ba lada bun eh ka um ha ka khyndew u
phan u lah ban pyut. Ki nongrep ha ri Khasi-Jaintia lehse kin
sngewlyngngoh ban tip ba ha Allahabad ki ju ai um (Irrigate)
haduh kumba hynriew sien ia u phan shi aiom; katta la jan
man ka ar taiew. Ki leh ia kane namar ka jaka ka rkhiang eh
bad kam ju ioh slap kumba ioh ha ri Khasi-Jaintia.
Ka rukom thung phan ha ri Khasi-Jaintia ruh ka iapher
shibun na ka rukom thung phan ha ri dkhar ne ri sepngi. Sha ri
dkhar bad ha ri sepngi ruh ki ju thung ha u lain ne ha ki lynter-
lynter (row) kiba jrong bha na ba sdang ha ba kut ka lyngkha.
Ki buh ia u phan symbai ha madan bad ki sa kynton da ka
khyndew na baroh ar liang. Ha ri Dkhar ki kynton ia kane ka
khyndew da ka lyngkor, kumta ka don ka nur ban ai um
hapdeng ar tylli ki lynter jong u phan. Ha ri Khasi-'Jaintia, bad
ha ri lum jong ka ri India baroh kawei, ki ju thung phan da ki
kynton kiba la pynmadan shuwa (raised bede)ii namar nga *
ngeit ba bun eh u slap bad kumta ka pynjulor eh ka iapiong'ha
39
ki rilum ban ia ha ki ri thor. Don kawei ka jingduna-ha ka rukom
rep phan ha ki ri lum mynta, bad kata ka long kato kaba nga la
pyni ha ka lynnong kaba mynshuwa, ba ka khyndew kaba na
jrong, kaba long kaba bha tarn na ka bynta ki jingthung baroh,
ka tuid lut sha ki ri thor. Kane ka jingleh jong ki nongrep ha ri
Khasi-Jaintia ha kine ki shispah snem ba la leit ka la pynlong

sa tang ki maw.

ai sboh ia ka lyngkha phan, la da ka sboh eit masi ne da kiwei
pat ki sboh kiba nga la pyni ha kajuh hi ka lynnong.

Uno uno u symbai uba don ka dak pyut dei ban kyntait noh

lah ban long kumba shi inshi ne shi inshi shiteng ka jingheh.

LYNNONG 10
U KBA
U kba u long uwei na ki jingthung kiba donkam tarn jong
ka pyrthei. U long mynta u jingbam uba kongsan jong ka ri
China, jong ki nong Japan, ki nong India bad kiwei kiwei ki
briew jong ka ri mihngi. Ha ri sepngi ruh ngi shem ba don
katto katne ka jingrep kba kum ha Italy, Spain bad ha ki bynta
kiba kham shit jong ka ri America. Hangne ha ka ri India ka
jingrep kba ka jur eh ha Andhra Pradesh, Punjab, Bengal, Tamil
Nadu, Bihar, Uttar Pradesh, Madhya Pradesh bad Assam.
U kba u long u jingthung uba mih bha ha ki jaka kiba kham
bun um. U kham met bha ha ki khyndew kiba skah (heavy
soil). La katta ruh lada ka lyngkha ka don ka um kaba biang,
bad lada ka don ki jingbam kiba biang na ka bynta u jingthung,
kata ki dewra ruh ki lah ban pynmih u kba uba itynnad.
Kumba nga la ong ha ka lynnong kaba mynshwa, don bun
jait ki jaid kba ha ka pyrthei. Don la kumno kumno kumba shi
phew hajar ne palat ki jait kba ha ka pyrthei baroh kawei, bad
ha ka ri India yn don palat ia ka san hajar ki jait kba. Kumta
nga ngeit ba ka ri Khasi-Jaintia ruh ka don shibun ki jait kba,
bad nga ngeit ruh ba ka lah ban ioh shuh kiwei ki jait kba na
kiwei pat ki bynta jong ka ri India kiba kham bha ban ia kiba
ka don mynta. Donkam namarkata ban pyrshang hi bun kiwei
pat ki jait kba haduh ban da shem ia ki jait kiba bha tarn na ka
bynta ka ri Khasi-Jaintia.
Ha ri Bengal bad Assam la pyniakyrpang (grouped) pat ia

kine ki jaid kba baroh kat kum ka rukom thung, ka por thung,
bad ka jingdonkam um jong ki. Don namarkata u jaid kba uba
ki khot u kba aus lane ahu uba ki shu bet belt ha ka pynthor
bad u aus ne ediu uba ki da sara. Ia utei uba nyngkong ki ju
thung ha Assam ha u bnai February, March bad April. Une u
jaid kba um da donkam um eh shibun. Ki ju ot ia u ha Assam
48
na u bnai June, July. la u kba aus ne ahu uba ki da sara, ki ju
thung ha ki jaka sara (nuMcry) ha u bnai April lane ha ka
taiew kaba nyngkong u May bad ki sa thung pat ha pynthor
hadien 20 haduh 30 sngi, bad ki sa ot pat ha u bnai August.
Don pat u jaid kba uba ki khot u kba sali uba ki ju sara ha u
bnai June-July bad ki thung pat ha pynthor ha u bnai July-
August bad ki ot ha u bnai November, December. Uwei pat u
jaid kba uba lah ban shem ha ka jylla Assam bad ha Bengal
ruh u long u kba aman. Une u long uto u kba uba ki thung ha
ki jaka ha kiba ka um slap ka lah ban tap ia ka pynthor haduh
kumba 10 haduh 15 phut ha ka jingjylliew. la une u kba ruh ki
JU thung naduh u bnai March haduh ka ba kut u May, bad ki ju
ot pat haduh u bnai December - January. Uwei pat u jaid kba
uba ki JU thung ha Assam u long uba ki khot boro. la une u kba
ki sara ha u bnai November bad ki sa thung pat ha ki bir ha u
bnai December-January, ki sa ot pat ha u bnai April - May.
Ki Rukom Thung Kba Ki Jingpang Bad Ki Khniang Kba:
Ha ki bynta jong ka ri Khasi-Jaintia kiba khap ri dkhar
(Assam) lehse lah ban thung ia kitei ki jaid kba baroh ha la ki

jaka jaka katkum ka jinglong jong ki jaid kba ne ka jaka bad
katkum ka jingbun jong ka um. Hynrei kiba kham bun na ki
kba ha ri Khasi-Jaintia ki long lehse tang ar jaid (kata ar tylli ki
group kumba ong ha ka ktien phareng). Kine ki long u kba lum
bad u kba pynthor. la u kba lum ki ju bet ha ki ranab jong ki
lum ynda ki la shyrti ia ki khlaw. Uba ar pat u jaid kba uba ki ju
kham thung ha ri Khasi-Jaintia u long u kba pynthor uba ki ju
bet ha ki hali kiba la ju shna bunsien ha ki them kiba long
hapdeng ar tylli ki lum. Kane ruh ka long ka rukom thung kba
ha ki lum jong ki paramarjan jong ngi ki Naga. Haba thung ha
ki ranab lum ia u kba ki shu thung kumto ha ka khyndew kaba
sngem da kaba ksam kumba shi inshi shiteng ne ar inshi hapoh
khyndew. Hynrei haba thung ha ka hali, lah ban leh kawei na
kine ar ki rukom (1) da kaba shu bet beit ha ki hali bad (2) da
kaba thung ha ki sara. Lada shu bet beit ha ki hali, kaba bha
eh ka long ban da pynspeh (sprout) ia u kba. Khnang ban
pynspeh ia u kba, ki ju buh ia u ha ki byrni bad ki sa pynngarti

Ki Jingbam Na U Kba :
Na u kba ngi ioh ia u khaw uba long u jingbam ba kongsan
tarn jong ki briew ha ki ri mihngi. Hangne ha ri India ki briew
kiba na sha ki thain mihngi, shatei lam mihngi bad shathie ki
long kiba bam bun eh u khaw ban ia kiba na sha kiwei pat ki
thain. Bad namar ba u Khasi-Pnar, uba dei na ki thain shatei
lam mihngi, u pyndonkam shibun ia khaw ha ki jingbam jong
u, kumta nga ngeit ba ka long kaba dei eh ia u nongrep Khasi-

Pnar ban sngewthuh bha aiu lah ban ioh na u kba bad na u
khaw lane na ki jingbam baroh ki ba shna na ki.
U khaw uba ngi ioh na ki mill (karkhana) kiba tyllait kba u
long uto uba la pyniakhlad ym tang ia u stait, hynrei uba ki la
shim noh katto katne ia i jinsop (pericarp) jong i symboh kba
bad i khmat kba (embryo lane germ), iba long i bynta i ba
don shibun ka jingbam kaba donkam na ka bynta ka jingkoit
jingkhiah jong u briew. U khaw dkhar (milled rice) namaikata
um da long uba bha eh na ka bynta ka met u briew kum u
khaw dung thlong. Dei na kata daw ba kiba bam khaw dkhar
bunsien ki shait ioh ia ka jingpang at (beriberi). La kumta ruh
u jait khaw baroh u duna ka kalsiom (calcium) ka met tynrai
kaba donkam shibun na ka bynta ka met u briew bad kaba
iarap ban tei ia ki shyieng.
Lada dung ia u kba ynda la lah pdem shuwa ha ka um
shipor bad sa shet ia u, kata ka jingkhlain jong u kum u jingbam
kam da duh eb. la u khaw uba ki shna ha kane ka rukom ki
khot u khaw snar lane, ha ka ha ka ktien phareng u parboiled
rice. Tang ka jingshnada une ruh ki siapher na kawei ka jaka

sha kawei pat. Ka nikom shna kaba suk eh ka lonQ kumne: la

shi srtgi ar sngi katkum u jaid kba. Hadien kata ki sa tiew ia

Ha Punjab pat ia une u khaw ki shna ha kane ka rukom

Nga sngew dei ruh ban pyni hangne ia u khaw ryngkew,

la u khoi uba ki shna na u kba ki shna kumne; la u kba ki

phareng glutenous rice. Kane ka ai jingmut ba une u khaw u

gluten bad kaba iapher na u starch. Hynrei ha ka jingshisha
um dei u gluten. U dei hi u starch tangba ha kawei pat ka
rukom lane ka dur.
Ha kaba kut ngi dei ban ong ba lada ka ri Khasi Jaintia ka
kwah ban rep ia u kba uba ka thmu ban die shabar ka ri, kata
ka dei ban rep da u kba uba rit symboh Ishir Ishir. Ha ri dkhar
ki khaw kiba rem ki dei kito kiba rit symboh (fine rice), bad
kito kiba heh symboh (bold rice) ki long kiba tad eh. Na kito
kiba rit symboh don pat katto katne ki jait kba kiba ki ju ia bch
eh ha kane ka ri India, kum u khaw kalma na Bengal lane u
khaw basmati na Dehradun. Ngi ngeit namarkata ka long kaba
myntoi ia u nongrep ha ri Khasi Jaintia ban pyrshang rep da u

khaw basmati, namar ba ka jinglong ka ri Khasi Jaintia ka ia
syriem ia ka Dehradun.

U RIEWHADEM
U riewhadem u long pat u jingthung ia u ba la wallam sha
kane ka ri India na America lehse ha ka por ba ka wan ka

la phriang kylleng ka pyrthei, ba mynta u long u jingthung uba
ki ju thung ha kylleng ki ri Jong ka pyrthei, kum ha ka United

shibun eh ki long kumne ter ter, ka Uttar Pradesh,' ka Bihar, ka
Punjab, ka Rajasthan, ka Gujarat, Tamil Nadu, ka Bengal bad
kiwei kiwei. Ka jylla Meghalaya kam da thung shibun kum kitei
ki jylla jong ka ri India lait tang ha ka ri Khasi - Jaintia. Kumta
ngi sngew dei ban niew ia une u jingthung kum uwei na ki
jingthung kiba kham donkam ha ki ri lum Khasi - Jaintia.
Ka Jlngiapoikha ki Riewhadem :

ki ia poikha kumne ki symboh riewhadem kim ju long bad dei

U DUMASLA

U Sohriew
U sohriew (Coix lacryma-Jobi) u long u uwei pat u
jingthung uba ki ju rep ha ri Khasi-Jaintia. Une u long u
jingthung uba kham jrong shibun ban ia kitei ki krai, ka rynieng
jong u ka long kumba haduh 6 phut. Une u jingthung ruh u
long hi u kynja krai (millet) kum kitei ki krai ba la thoh haneng
tangba u kham heh symboh. Ha ka jingdonkam jong u kum u
jingbam ruh u ia syriem ia kitei ki krai, la um da long lehse uba
da bha eh kum kitei ki krai haneng.
Ki Dai, U Rymbaija bad U Rymbol-Ktung
Kiwei pat ki jingthung kiba nga kwah ban thoh lyngkot ha

kane ka lynnong ki long ki kynja dai, u dai rahor (Cajanua
caJun) u dai masur (Lens esculanta) u dai shana (Cicer

(Pisum arvense), rymbaija (Phaseolus calcaratua) rymbai
ktung (Glycine max).
U dai rahor u long u dai uba ngi shem bha kylleng ka ri
India bad uba long ruh ha ki katto katne ki jaka them jong ka ri
Khasi-Jaintia. Une u long u jingthung uba long man la ka snem
(annual), hynrei, u lah ruh ban neh ar snem ne palat ruh. Ha
ki jaka ha kiba kham bun u slap u long u jingthung uba piar
bha ki tnat, kumta ba dei ban thung ia u ha ka jingjngai kumba
lai phut na uwei ha uwei pat u lain, hynrei, ha ki jaka kiba
kham duna ka um ki thung marjan ia u. Ka jingjan jingjngai ka
shong ruh ha la ka jait ka jait. Don ki jaid rahor kiba piar ki
tnat bad don ruh kiba shu long beit. La une u jingthung u
donkam katto katne ka um ha ka por ba u san, la katta ruh um
da donkam shibun eh ka um. Dei namarkata ka daw, ba um
da long bha eh ha ki jaka bun slap jong ka ri Khasi-Jaintia. Ha
ri dkhar, ha ki nongkyndong, ia u symboh ki syang ha ka ding
shuwa ban tylliat ia u ha u maw tylliat. Habadei badei ki da
kyllan bad ka umphniang bam, shuwa ban tylliat ia u khnang
ba un ym Iwet eh haba tylliat hynrei, tangba kan shu khuid ka
snep bad ba u symboh un pait arliang. Une u jingthung u lortg
ruh uba ki shait thung ban pynbha ia ka khyndew, liargar u

long na ka iingkur (family) jong kito ki jingthung kiba nga la

ka ktien phareng. U long mh u jingthung uba don ki thied kiba
khlain bha kiba kdi ban kynroi wat ia ka khyndew kaba dait

eh. Une u jingthung u lah nih ban pyniap-op (smother) wat

U dai saw lane u dai mzisur (Lens esculanta) uba ki khot ha

U Dai shana (Cicer arietinum) uba ki khot ha ka ktien

U Rymbaiktung (Glycine max) u long u jingthung uba don

na u symboh jong une u jingthung. La ong ruh ba ki pynmih

Ha kaba kut ngan shu ong kyllum ba ki tei baroh ki long ki

LYNNONG 16
U TYMPEW
U tympew (Piper betel) u long u jingthung u ba wan lehs^
na Java bad u ba la wanrah sha kane ka ri jong ngi lehse da ki
Portuguese. la une mynta ki thung ha bun ki jaka ka ri India,
kum ka Maharastra, Bengal, Assam bad kiwei kiwei ki jylla.
Ha Assam imat ki kham thung bha ha ka Surma Valley bad ha
Meghalaya ha ki phang Riwar jong ka ri Khasi-Jaintia.
U tympew u long bha ha ka khyndew kaba sbur bha, kata
ka khyndew ka bym da dait eh, bad kaba don shibun ki pyut
jong ki dieng ki sla (dead organic matter). Kadei ruh ban
long ka khyndew ha kaba ka um kam da neh slcm eh, la u
jingthung u donkam um man ka por. U long bha ruh ha ki jaka
kiba shit bad ba sngem katta kum ki jaka Riwar, bad ki jaka
dymmiew. Lada ka Khyndew ka kham duna ka sboh lane katei
ka pyut jong ki dieng bad ki sla (humus) lah ban ai sboh da ka
eit masi lane da ki jingthung kiba lah ban puh kylla kum da u
rymbaija ne uno uno u jingthung uba iadei kur bad u. Haba

thung ha ri ranab ruh, dei ban sumar ba ka khyndew kan ym
tuid lehnohei sha ri thor. Kumba nga la ong ha ka lynnong
kaba mynshuwa, kane ka khyndew kaba na jrong kalong ka
sboh kaba bha eh ia ki jingthung baroh.
Ynda la lur ne puh ia ka khyndew, hap ban shna ki nur bad
ki kynton ha ka jingjngai kumba shi mitar shiteng haduh ar
mitar. la u tympew ki sa thung ha ka jingjngai kumba 15 cm ha
kine ki kynton. KI nur ki iarap ban kit noh ia ka um kaba bun
eh. Namar ba u tympew u long kum u jyrmi, kumta donkam
ban da thung da ki dieng ban kynshan ia u. Ha ki ri thor ki ju
kyrshan ia u tympew da ki shken. la ki thning kiba ki
pyndonkam ban thung ki ioh na ki lyngkha tympew ki barim.
Ki ot lyngkhot lyngkhot ia ki jyntang jong u tympew bad ki da
ieh kumba hynriew mat ha uwei uwei u thning. Ki juh dkh^t ia

ki lai tylli ki sla kiba sharud bad ki ieh pat lai tylli kiba shaneng
haba thung ia ki. la ki kliar jong u jyntang ki bret noh. Lah ban
thung ha kano kano ka por kaba syiad bad haba ka khyndew
ka ioh ka um kaba biang. Ynda la thung donkam ban ai um
(irrigate) bunsien ia u, hynrei ym dei ban ioh paiat eh ka um.
Ynda ki la kham heh khyndiat lah ban ai sboh ia ki da ka sboh
shaba jong u symbai iyrso lane da kano kano ka sboh shaba

jingpang da kaba bet ia u tutia ha ka khyndew lane da kaba

Ka jin^dieit tympew ka sdang kumba lai ne saw bnai hadien

LYNNONG 17
U KWAI

U Kwai (Areca catechu) u dei u jingthung uba long ban
rep tang ha ki jaka kiba shit sngem. la u kwai ki briew ha ri
India ki la nang ban pyndonkam la bun bun snem ym tang ha
kaba bam kai bam myllen hynrei, ki ju pyndonkam ia u ha ka
leh naim leh rukom.
Ia ka jaka tynrai na kaba une u jingthung u mih nyngkong
ym lah ban pynthikna tang ba imat dei hi ha kine ki thain Asia
khamtam kito ki jaka kiba harud duriaw. Ka ri India ka pynmih
haduh kumba ar lakh ton u kwai ha ka shisnem na kumba
arphew lakh san phew hajar hektar.
Ka Rep Kwai
U kwai u long u jingthung uba jrong bha bad ki sla jong u
ki mih tang na kliar. Ngi khot ia kine ki jait dieng ki palm. Ka
jingjrong jong une u dieng ka pher na kawei ka jaka sha kawei
pat, bad u lah ban jrong haduh 90 phut (27mitars).
U dieng kwai u mih ha ri them bad ha ri lum, tangba ynda
thung ia u sha ki jaka kiba kham jrong um da sei soh bha
shuh. Ka jaka rep ia une u jingthung ki dei ban long ki jaka ha
kiba ka jingshit jingkhriat ha ka lyiur bad ka tlang kam da ia
pher than. U dieng kwai u sei soh bha ha ki jaka kiba ka jingshit
ka long hapdeng ka 15°C haduh 38°C.
U Kwai u mih ha ki jaid khyndew bapher bapher, naduh ki
khyndew laterite bad khyndew loam jong ka ri India ba shathie,
ki khyndew alluvial jong ka Assam, Benged haduh ki khyndew
colluvial kiba ngi shem sha ki thain war ka ri Khasi Jaintia.
Kaba kongsan ka long ba ki bri ki dei ban don ka khyndew
kaba jylliew bad ha kaba ka um kan ym neh slem eh. Kan bha
shibun lada don ki dieng kiba jrong, ban iada ia u dieng kwai
na ka jingthang palat ka sngi khamtam na ka liang lam sepngi.
96

Ban buh symbai ia u kwai, ngi donkam ban da jled ia u
kwai na ki dieng kiba ju kham soh kloi bad seisoh bha. Dei nih
ban da jied da ki kwai kiba la ih bha bad ban kyntait noh ia
kito kiba thoh dak bad ba sniewdur. U kwai uba ki ia jted janai
kumtei ki ju thung shwa ha ki *p 0 r sara lane norsari.
Ha Bengal, Orissa bad ki ri India ba shathie, shwa ba kin
thung ia u kwai ha ki norsari, ki ju pynspeh shwa ia u kumne.
Ki tap ia u kwai da u shyiap ha ki jaka kiba kham tngen bad ki
ai um ia u barabor (irrigate) bad hadien sawphew sngi ei ei u
kwai u sdang speh. Hadien lai bnai lane ynda, u la sdang slap
kin sa rah tynrai noh pat sha ki jaka norseri kaba ki la pynkhreh
bad ai sboh bha bad ki thung pat ia u ha ki jingjngai kaba 30
cm (shiphut) na uwei sha u wei pat. la kine ki norsari ki da
shna de da ki jingker ban da ia u jingthung na ka jingtangon
eh ka sngi. Ynda dap khadar haduh khadphra bnai ki rah ban
thung noh pat sha ki bri.
Sha ri jong ngi, ki nongrep kwai ki jied ia ki jaka kiba kham
sngem bad ki sara beit ia u kwai hangto khlem da pynspeh
shwa ia u. Ki ieh ia u hangto haduh kumba lai snem ei ei bad
ki sa rah tynrai ban thung pat ha ki bri ha ki bnai slap. Naba
peit thuh ia u dieng uba kham bun mat ha u dieng met jong u
hadien ba la thung ngi lah ban batai ba un long u dieng u ban
kham sei soh.
Ka por ba bha tarn ban thung ia u kwai ka long ha ki bnai
slap kata ha ki bnai June-July. Lada ioh slap kham kloi lah ruh
ban thung ia u ha u bnai May. Shwa ban thung, ki ju tih ia ki

thliew kaba jylliew kumba 60 cm. (2phut) sawdong bad ki buh
ia u dieng ha pdeng bad ki sa pyndap khyndew. Dei ruh ban
da khem bha ia ka khyndew na baroh sawdong. la ka jingjngai
na uwei u dieng sha uwei pat ki ju pynlait kumba 2.70 mt.
(9phut).
Kham bunsien ki jaka rep ia une u jingthung ki long ki jaka
baheh slap, bad kumta ki khyndew kirn da long kiba sboh
namar ka jingtuid ka khyndew. Kumta khnang ba ngin ioh ia
ka jingmih bad jingseisoh ka ban iai neh, ngi dei ban ai ia ki
jingbam kiba u jingthung u donkam. la kane ka jingbam feih
97
ban ai lyngba ki sboh nuit, sboh jingri bad na ki sboh fertiljzer

U dieng kwai u shim por bun snem haduh kumba hynniew
lane phra snem ba un sdang ban soh. Khnang ban yn nang

Ki Khnlang bad Jingpang Kwai
Na ka liang u khniang, u kwai ym da don eh kita ki ban da
pynjulor than eh ia u dieng. Kiba ju kham iohi ki long ki kynja
khniang kiba rit (scale insects) kiba kjit ia u dieng bad uba ki
khot u arecanui beetle {Araecerus faatculatua) uba bam
ia ki sla bad ruh ia ki soh.
Ki don ruh katto katne ki jingpang kiba ktah ia u diengkwai.
Ka tram sla lane ka leaf blight (Contothyrim area) ka dei ka
jingpang kaba pynthoh dak ia ki sla bad kito ki sla ki hap noh
sha khyndew. Ka jingpang kwai kaba kham pynjulor ia ki bri
ka dei arecanut bud rot disease {Phytophthora
paimivora). Kane ka jingpang ka la sdang ban kynrei bha ha
ri Khaisi. Kane ka jingpang ka pynpyut ia u shylluit uba mih
hapdeng, bad hadien u ktah lut ia u dieng baroh uwei. U dieng
uba la kem pang u sma iewtung bha. Kane ka jingpang ka

kham toi hadien ba la slap, haba ka bneng ka lyhop bha. Ka
kham duna ha ki por tlang bad ha ki por jur slap. Ban iada na
kane ka jingpang lah ban pyndonkam da ka dawai kaba ki
khot ka metalaxyl. Synreit lane spray ia kane ka dawai da
kaba pyndonkam da ka foot sprayer ar sien shi snem, shisien
shwa ki bnai slap kata ha ki bnai Match bad shisien ha ki bnai
September -October, hadien ki bnai slap.
Ngi dei ban kynmaw bha ba ngi dei ban synreit ia kane ka
dawai la kumno kumno ar bnai shwa ban kheit ia u kwai.
U kwai u sdang phuh syntiew naduh ki bnai February-May
bad u soh u sdang ih pat naduh ki bnai November-December
katkum ki jedta jaka.
Haba u sdang ih, u kwai u kylla na ka rong jyrngam sha ka
rong stem bad ynda u la ih bha u kylla rong saw. Ka aiom kheit
98
kwai ka sdang naduh u bnai December bad ka neh haduh u
bnai March - May.
Kiba bun hi ha ri India kim ju bam ia u kwai im uba ki khot
u katcha supari hynrei ki kham bam da u kwai uba la thad
tyrkhong uba ki khot u chali supari. Ngi pat ha ri Khasi ngi
bam wai-im ha ki bnai tlang bad u waiskop ha ki bnai lyiur. Ha
ki thain Garo Hills, ki don kiba bam da u kwai uba dang lung
bad dang jyrngam. Imat u lah ban don la ka jong ka jingmad.
Ban shna ia u chali- supari, ki ju thad rkhiang ha madan ba
la siang dew bilat haduh ba un da tyrkhong bha, bad ka um ha
u kwai ka sah satang kumba 10 na ka 100 (10%).
U Khasi hyndai, uba stad, u la lap ia ka buit ban buh ia u
kwai ha um bad ban pynkylla ’waiskop ia u. la kane ka rukom
pdem kwai ngi shem imat tang hangne ha ri Khetsi. Ki pdem ia

u kwai ki ju shna da ki pukhri kiba jylliew, teng teng haduh 4
m. (12phut). Ki thep ia u kwai ha ki kriah kiba la shna kyrpang
da ki siej bad ki sa pdem ia ka ha kine ki pukhri ba la pyndap
um. Ki pdem ia u kumne haduh san bnai lane haduh ban da
lut u ‘wai-im.
Nalor u soh uba ngi bam, kiwei kiwei ki bynta jong une u
jingthung ruh ki long kiba myntoi. la u snep kwai lah ban shna
kot bad Card-board. Na u kwai u bym pat ih, lah ban ioh ia ka
tannin ka dawai kaba ki pyndonkam ha ki karkhana shna snieh.
la ka dop-wai ki pyndonkam haba song jin^ong bad ia u dieng
met pat ki pyndonkam haba shna iing shna sem. Nga ngeit ba
ka por ka la poi ba ngi dei ban sdang wad jingtip shuh shuh
shaphang ki rukom ia une u jingthung khnang ba ngin kham
ioh myntoi da kaba rep ia u.

LYNNONG 18
U SLASHA
U Slasha u long uwei pat u jmgthung uba nga ngeit u iah
ban roi shibun ha ri Khasi-Jaintia. Une u long u jingthung uba
ki thung bha ha Assam, Datjeeling bad ki jaka shaphang shathie
ka ri India. La shem ba u dieng slasha u mih hi ha ki khlaw ki

nyngkong eh ha ki snem 1977-78 ha Umsning bad ha Riangdo.

pynshlur ia ki nongrep lyngba ka jingpyniaid ka Department

U KOPHI
U Kophi u long u jingthung uba dang wan shen sha ri Khasi-
Jaintia bad hapdeng kine ki khyndiat snem ki nongrep ki la
ithuh bha ia u. Hynrei ka jingrep kophi imat kam da roi stet
kumba ngi khmih lynti. Nga ngeit namarkata, ba kane ka

kophi ha ka ri lum Khasi-Jaintia Jong ngi.
U Kophi imat u long u jingthung uba la wallam ba ka ri
Africa ha kaba ngi shem bun jaid (species) ki kophi. Ki Kophi
ha kata ka ri ki mih ha ka khlaw ka btap. Don ruh u jaid kophi
u ba dei uba mih nyngkong eh ha kane ka ri Khasi-Jaintia ia
uba ki khot u Coffea khastana. la une la shem ba u don ha
ka shnong Nongwar, sha thain Sohra. Hynrei na kine ki kophi

kiba bun jaid, don ar jaid kiba ki ju thung eh na ka bynta ba
kin shna ia u kophi uba ki pyndonkam na ka bynta ka jingdih,
kine ki ar jaid ki long (1) Coffee robusta bad (2) Coffee
arabica. Une u ba nyngkong u long uba kham met bah ban ia
uba ar, u lah ban jrong naduh kumba 15 haduh 25 phut bad
ka met (trunk) ka lah ban long haduh kumba 8 inshi, na
shiliang ha shiliang (diameter). Hynrei haba rep ia u kata dei
ban ialeh ban pynneh ia ka jingjrong jong u hapoh hynriew
phut. Ka rukom san jong u arabica ka long kat kum ka jaka,
lada ka long kaba lait na ki dieng bah kata u piar bha ki tnat,
bad ki tnat ruh ki mih naduh jan khyndew. Hynrei ha ki jaka
kiba kham shah ki diengbah, une u kophi u lah ban don tang
uwei u met bah uba jrong tang kumba 6 phut ne kham palat
ruh. Tangba ha ba rep ia u arabica ki ju pynneh ia ka jingjrong
jong u tang hapoh lai phut. Ki symboh jong u kophi robusta
kim da heh eh kat ki jong u kophi arabica hynrei, ka jingmih
na une u kdphi ka khatn palat ban ia kaba na u kophi arabica.
U kophi uba ki ioh na u arabica pat, imat u kham bha ban ia u
robusta. U robusta imat u kham lait ruh na ki jingpang bad na
104
ki khniang kiba pynjot ia u, hynrei u kham iap kloi ban ia u

U kophi u long bha ha ki jaka kiba ka jingjrong (altitude)

iarap shibun ban pynmih soh kham bun. Kaba leh kumne ka
iapher na kaba thung ia u jingthung uba tap ia ka khyndew

phlangJanfiLkano kano kaba ki pyndonkam ban tap kumne ki

uba nga la thoh ha ka lynnong kaba khadlai. Utei u kew uba
ha ka lynnong kaba khadlai u dei na ka ing kur jong u kba, u
barli, ki krai, bad kiwei kiwei ki jaid phlang. Hynrei, u kew
Nepal um dei na ka iingkur ki phlang (Gramineae family),
hynrei u dei na ka iingkur u rubarb, u jarain bad kiwei kiwei ki
Jinghtung kiba iapher bha na ki phlang. La khot buckwheat
ia u lehse namar ba ki German ki pyndonkam ia u kum u kew
bad namar ba ka dur jong u shyieng ka long kum ki symbai
jong u dieng beech, bad kumta ki khot ia u, u buckweisen ha
ka ktien German, haba la kylla sha ka phareng buckwheat.
Dei na kane ka daw lehse ba ki Khasi ruh ki khot ia une u
jingthung u kew. Nga sngew dei ban khot ia u, u kew Nepal
namara ba la wallam nyngkong ia u sha ri Khasi da ki shipai
kiba wan na Nepal bad namar ba nga kwah ban pyniapher ia
u na u kew uba shisha. Imat don ar jaid (species) ki kew Nepal
ha ka pyrthei kiba ki kham thung bha: uwei na ki, ki khot
Fagopyrum eaculentum bad uwei pat uba ki khot

imat u long uba ki rep ha Germany bad kiwei kiwei ki ri Europe

rit. U Star George Walt u ong ba u tartaricum u long u jingbam

tylliat.

Ki Khniang Sohniamtra
U Sohniamtra u don katto katne ki khniang kiba pynshitom
ia u, hynrei kiba kham pynshitom eh ia u imat ki dei kine (i) u

ka met Ki thapniang ki long kiba liehbthuh (grey) bad kiba

na ka khyndew.

Ki Jingpang Sohniamtra
U dieng sohniamtra u don ruh bun jaid ki jingpang, hynrei

kiba ju kham shem ha kane ka ri ki long kine : (i) ka pangiap

kumne, ki phareng ki khot ia kane ka jingpang anthracnose.

Lada kine ki syntiew ki hap ki don ki jingthoh dak kiba saw ha
ki, kata hgi lah ban ngeit ba dei kabe ka juh ka jingpang kaba
pynhap ia ki. Haba ki syntiew ki hap kumne ki phareng ki khot

mangkariang ka lah pat ban pynpaw jingpang tang ha ki sla.

bthuh stem bad kiba pyllun. Ynda kine ki jingthoh ki la kham
skm, lah ban iohi ha kita ki kynja met (substance) kiba rit bin

ka paw> tang ha ka sla kumne ki khot ha ka ktien phareng

kane ka jingpang. la kane ka dawai ki pynum ha ka um bad ]ki

Ka jingpang mih thit (Phytophthora parasitica) kaba ki
