U PHAN DIENG
U Phandieng, uba ki khot ha ka ktien phareng Tapioca
lane Cassava (Manthot eaculenta), u long u jingthung uba
bha ha ka jaka kiba shitsngem. Une u long u jingthung uba ki
thung bha ha ki jaka kiba syrietn ia ka ri Bhoi, kumta ngi shem
ba u long u jingbam uba ki ju bam bha ha Kerala (South India)
bad ha Malaysia. Une u jingthung tnat u wan na Brazil ha South
America, la une u jingthung mynta ha ri India ki shu thung
tang ban shu bam ha iing ha sem, hynrei ia u starch uba ioh na
une u jingthung lah ban pyndonkam ha ki bun ki rukom.
Une u jingthung u long uba jrong kumba hynriew haduh
khadphra phut, katkum u jaid. Ki sla jong u ki long kiba pait
pnat (lobcd) . Ka mat dieng jong u ka long kaba pait tnat shibun,
bad ki tnat ki long kiba rit, bad ka rong jong ka snep dieng ka
lah ban long kaba jyrngam, lane kaba jyrngam lam lieh, lane
kaba saw lane lam saw katkum u jaid u jaid. Une u jingthung
u don ki thied, syriem ia u phan, kiba bit ban bam. Kiije ki
thied ki shim por naduh hynriew bnai haduh khadphra bnai
ban ih, kata la u jaid u jaid.
U ki don bun jaid ki phandieng hynrei la ju kham pyniapher

ia ki baroh tang ha ki ar rukom, uwei uba thiang bad uwei pat

Lane ban bam kum u phan myngngor lane u phan karo, lane
lah ban ot stang ia u bad hadien kata sa shet malu mala bad sa
pynrkhiang ha ka sngi bad hadien kata pat sa sdieh ia u.
Kumba 16 haduh 26 bynta na ka shispah jong une u phan
u dei u starch. Don katto katne ki jaid kiba lah ban don haduh
40 bynta na ka shispah. Khnang ban ioh ia une u starch, ki
don ki karkhana kiba heh ha Malaysia bad ha Java kiba tylliat
ia une u phan ban pynmih ia une u starch.
Une u jingthung u don ruh katto katne ki jingpang bad ki
khniang kiba pynjulor ia u. Kawei na ki jingpang kaba ki ju
shem ha une u jingthung ka long ka jingpang sla (Cercospera
cassavae). Lah ban kham pynduna ia kane ka jingpang da

kaba kynshait da ka Bordeaux mixture. Na ki khniang kiba
bam ia une u jingthung, kawei ka long ka khniang kaba ki khot
ha ka ktien phareng ka scale insect {Aonidomytllus albua).
Kane ka khniang ruh ka bam ia ki sla. Lah ban pynduna ia
kane ka khniang da kaba kynshait da ka kynja, umphniang
sharak (kerosine emulsion). Kawei pat ka khniang kaba bam
ia ki sla jong une u jingthung ka long ka khniang kaba rit katta
katta bad ka long kaba shitom ban iohi ia ka. Kane ka shait
shong tang hapoh ka sla. Ynda kane ka khniang ka la jyllei
bha, ki sla ki shu hap hi. Ki khot ia kane ka khniang ha ka ktien
phareng red spider {Tetranychua ap.). Lah ban kham
pynduna ia kane ka khniang da kaba kynshait da ka shun kaba
khleh bad u kyndok.

KALENDAR OF OPERATION HABA REPSOHNIAMTRA
Kat kumba ka long hi haduh mynta, ka rep sohniamtra
hangne ha ri Khasi-Jaintia kam pat long kaba da biang thikna.
Bun na ki nongrep jongngi ki. rep da ki spah bad hajar tynrai,
hynrei kim pat nang ban sumar ia ki ha ka rukom kaba dei
khnang ban ioh ka jingmih kaba biang. Bunsien ki shu ei bcit
kumto kumba thung ia ki dieng khlaw.
Namar kata ka long kaba kham bha ia ki nongrep ban rep
tang katkum ba lah ka bor bad ban sumar bha ia ki dieng soh
ha ka rukom kaba biang. Ka jingrep sohniamtra ka don ki rukom
sumar kiba u dei ban leh baroh shi snem. Kata ka long naduh
ba sdang u snem da kaba pynkuid ia ki nuit ki nier ia ka bri,

pushara ia u dieng met bad kumta ter ter haduh ban da kheit,
jied bad die ia u soh. ia ki kam ba dei ban leh khnang ba ka
jingrep sohniamtra kan ai ka jingmyntoi, ngi lah ban shna da
ka kynja kalendar ba ki khot ka kalendar of operations kumne
harum :
Bnai : Ki jingtrei
Kyllalyngkot Pyndep ia ka jing kheit soh lada ym pat dcp.
Shna lane pynbha ia ki half moon terrace.
Pynkhuid bad malt khlaw ha bri.
Rymphang Pyndep ia ki jingshna bad jingpynbha ia ki
terrace bad jingpynkhuid ia ka bri. Pynkhuid
ia ki sohpailen ba mih ha ka met bah. Weng
bad ot noh ia ki tnat ba la iap bad kiba i don
jingpang. Ai shun (da ka shun byrni) ia ka
khyndew ha trai ki dieng kumba SOOgm shi
tynrai.
Lber Pyndep ia ka Jingpynkhuid sohpailen bad jingai
shun. Pushara ia u dieng met da ka bordeaux
paste naduh trai haduh kumba lai phut
shaneng. Ai sboh, ai dawai sboh da ki Urea,
SSP MOP kumne : 325gm : 725gm: 300gm
shi tynrai.
latpng : Pyndep ia ka jing pushara bad ai sboh . Synreit
ia ki micronutrients kum u zinc sulphate
manganese sulphate bad borax ha ka jingkhleh
kumne lOOOgm : lOOOgm : 200gm ha ka
arspah litar ka um-kane kan dap na ka bynta
80-100 tylli ki dieng. Synreit da ki hormones
ban iada na ka jinghap ki soh kum ka 2-4-D
Jymmang : Pyndep ia ka jingsynreit micronutrients bad
hormones Synriet ki dawai khniang kumka
demecron bad ka dawai carbofuran ha ki
tynrai. Synreit ruh ia ki dawai iada na ki
jingpang kum ka bordeaux mixture lane copper
fungicide bad sulphur fungicide.
Jylliew : Pyndep ia ki jingai dawai. Ai sboh biang bad
ki sboh dawai kumba leh ha u bnai Rymphang
ne Lber (shi paketka urea, shi paket shiteng ka
SSP bad shi paket ka MOP ka dap na ka bynta
80-100 tylli ki dieng soh).
Naitung : Pyndep ia ka jing ai sboh bad dawai sboh
Synreit biang ia ki dawai khniang/jingpang Iada
i donkam.

Nailur ; Pyndep ia kitei ki kam haneng bad synreit biang
ia ki micronutrients kum ka Zinc, Manganese
bad Boron.
Nailar ; Pyndep ia ka jingsynreit dawai khniang/
jingpang bad micro nutrients. Pynkhuid biang
ia ka bri.
Risaw . : Pyndep ia ka jing pynkhuid bri bad sdang ia
ka jingkheit soh.
Nawieng : Kheit, jied bad die ia ki soh.
Nohprah : Kheit, jied bad die ia ki soh bad sdang pynkhuid
bri.
NB : Kane haneng ka long tang ka nuksa. Ban shna kham ia
kane ka kalendar kaban ia dei bad ka jaka wad jingmut
na kito ki ba trei hapoh ka department jong ka Agriculture.
